    📄 摘要：FeTe 作为铁基超导体中的代表性母体材料，为探索轨道选择性关联、磁性和非常规超导性之间的相互作用提供了理想平台。然而，应变作用下 FeTe 的关联电子结构与磁性仍缺乏统一图景。在本研究中，我们结合密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论以及 Heisenberg 模型分析，揭示了关联电子结构的轨道选择性重构，并发现 FeTe 中存在一条由应变驱动的磁性演化路径：从双列反铁磁序演化为条纹反铁磁序，其间经过一个中间的竞争性错列 n 聚体反铁磁区域。适度应变会产生一个更具相干性的准粒子与受抑制的局域磁矩共存的区域。进一步施加应变会将 FeTe 推入不相干的关联区域，其中局域磁矩保持稳健，且低能态由 Fe-3d_z² 轨道主导。这些结果确立了 FeTe 中跨越不同磁性态和关联电子态的应变驱动演化路径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26652v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: FeTe, as a representative parent material among iron-based superconductors, provides an ideal platform for exploring the interplay among orbital-selective correlations, magnetism, and unconventional superconductivity. However, a unified picture of the correlated electronic structure and magnetism of FeTe under strain remains to be fully clarified. Here, combining density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory and Heisenberg model analysis, we uncover an orbital-selective reconstruction of the correlated electronic structure and reveal a strain-driven trajectory from bicollinear to stripe antiferromagnetism (AFM) via an intermediate competing staggered n-mer AFM regime in FeTe. Moderate strain gives rise to a regime where more coherent quasiparticles coexist with suppressed local moments. Further strain drives FeTe into an incoherent correlated regime with robust local moments and Fe-3dz^₂-dominated low-energy states. These results establish a strain-driven trajectory across distinct magnetic and correlated electronic states in FeTe.

    💡 亮点：该工作把 DFT+DMFT 的轨道分辨关联电子结构与 Heisenberg 模型结合，考察应变驱动的 FeTe 磁序变化。核心结论是应变引发轨道选择性重构，并伴随双共线磁序向条纹磁序演化。给定摘要没有报告临界应变、轨道占据、质量重整化或交换常数，因此定量强度尚不能判断。它与团队的自旋哈密顿量、磁性、应变调控和强关联材料设计方向有明确方法交叉。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：工作将应变下FeTe的关联电子结构置于DFT+DMFT框架中处理，并以Heisenberg模型分析磁相互作用与双共线到条纹磁序的演化。其核心是先从电荷和轨道分辨的电子结构识别轨道选择性重构，再把低能磁性归约为交换作用竞争；这比只用静态DFT磁总能比较更能保留局域关联。摘要未报告单轴或双轴应变范围、U和J、温度、杂质求解器、双计数、交换参数和临界应变，任何定量相图都仍需查正文。

    🔗 相关工作关联：FeTe连接铁基超导体中Hund金属、轨道选择性Mott物理、应变调控磁性和非常规超导的长期问题。相对DFT+U，DFT+DMFT引入频率依赖自能，可处理准粒子重整化与轨道分化；相对纯Heisenberg模型，它先由关联电子结构约束交换图像。题干未说明五位研究者各自方向，无法准确点名既有成果；方法上可与团队的DMFT、二维磁体应变工程、DFT+U和机器学习势工作形成可操作的接口。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可在CrSBr、CrI3等层状磁体建立应变构型库，先用含SOC的DFT+U计算不同磁序、各向异性能和交换常数，再对关联最强的轨道子空间实施电荷自洽DFT+DMFT。训练集应覆盖面内拉压、层间距与滑移，温度点覆盖磁转变附近；以ARPES或光谱可比的谱函数、磁化易轴、Raman声子及中子/磁光交换尺度交叉验证。对FeTe本身应复现实验双共线与条纹区域后，再判断机器学习代理模型能否插值自能和相界。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题面内双轴拉伸如何同时重塑 FeTe 的低能轨道电子结构、局域磁矩和磁基态？尤其是，双共线反铁磁如何沿着怎样的应变路径演化到条纹反铁磁，并与超导相关窗口关联？
    创新方法把 DFT+DMFT 与 J1-J2-J3 Heisenberg 模型联用，建立应变相图；关键突破是识别出轨道选择性重构这一 Aha 点：拉伸不只是改带宽，而是让低能态从 Fe-3d x2-y2 / dxy 逐步转向 Fe-3d z2 主导，同时改写磁交换竞争。
    工作流程输入为不同面内晶格常数下的 FeTe 结构；先用 DFT+DMFT 计算谱函数、自能、费米面、局域自旋响应和多重态，再做总能比较得到 NM、FM、Néel、stripe、bicollinear、Dimer、Trimer 的能量排序，最后映射到 J1-J2-J3 模型，得到从双共线 AFM 经竞争性 n-mer 区到条纹 AFM 的应变轨迹。
    关键结果中等拉伸先增强准粒子相干性、削弱局域磁矩，并进入 Dimer/Trimer 近简并的强磁竞争区；更大拉伸则压制全部 3d 低能谱重，出现非费米液体散射，低能态由 Fe-3d z2 主导，磁基态最终转为 stripe AFM。
    应用价值为 FeTe 的应变工程提供微观图景，解释薄膜中磁性抑制与超导窗口为何同时出现；提示非常规超导并非来自磁性简单消失，而是来自相干准粒子、强磁涨落和轨道重构共存的临界区域。

    第一部分：核心概览

    FeTe 在铁基超导母体材料中具有特殊地位：块体基态为双共线反铁磁序，而非铁砷族常见的条纹反铁磁序。该研究通过 DFT+DMFT 与 J1-J2-J3 Heisenberg 模型结合，建立了拉伸应变下 FeTe 的统一相图：中等面内双轴拉伸增强准粒子相干性、削弱局域磁矩，并进入交错 n-mer 反铁磁竞争区；更大拉伸则诱导轨道选择性重构，低能态转为 Fe-3d z2 主导，同时磁基态演化为条纹反铁磁序。创新点在于把实验观察到的应变诱导超导、磁性抑制与轨道重构纳入同一微观框架，指出超导相关窗口并非简单磁性消失，而是相干电子态与强磁竞争共存。

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《Strain-driven orbital-selective reconstruction and bicollinear-to-stripe evolution in FeTe》（None，）

    背景：FeTe 是铁基超导代表性母体材料，块体基态为双共线 AFM，与多数铁砷化物的条纹 AFM不同。近期 FeTe 薄膜实验显示，去除过量间隙 Fe 并施加面内拉伸后可出现超导，同时伴随 Fe-3d x2-y2 与 Fe-3d z2 之间的谱重转移。

    方法：使用 DFT+DMFT 计算顺磁四方 FeTe 在不同面内晶格常数下的谱函数、自能、费米面、局域自旋响应与局域多重态；使用 DFT 总能量比较 NM、FM、Néel、stripe、bicollinear、Dimer、Trimer 构型；再映射到 J1-J2-J3 Heisenberg 模型。

    结果：从 a=3.81 到 4.01 Å，中等拉伸增强费米能级附近谱重和准粒子相干性；到 a=4.22 Å，全部 Fe-3d 轨道低能谱重受抑，出现非费米液体散射和 Fe-3d z2 主导低能态。磁性从 a=3.81 Å 的 bicollinear AFM，经 a=3.86-4.01 Å 的 staggered n-mer 竞争区，最终在 a=4.11/4.22 Å 进入 stripe AFM 区。

    讨论：超导相关应变窗口对应强磁竞争 + 相干准粒子，不同于空穴掺杂铜氧化物中从 Mott 绝缘相到过掺杂费米液体的路径。

    局限：主要为理论计算；未直接计算超导配对强度、Tc 或薄膜界面效应；应变类型集中于面内双轴拉伸，单轴应变、压力和局域应变仍待检验。

    结论：面内双轴拉伸是调控 FeTe 中 Hund 金属关联、非费米液体行为、轨道选择性重构与磁相竞争的有效旋钮。

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析

    Abstract

    Unified strain trajectory：核心命题是把 FeTe 的关联电子结构和磁相演化放入同一应变控制框架，明确采用 DFT+DMFT 与 Heisenberg model analysis，直接目标是厘清应变下 FeTe 仍未统一的电子-磁性图像；关键原句为 *"However, a unified picture of the correlated electronic structure and magnetism of FeTe under strain remains to be fully clarified."*

    Orbital-selective reconstruction：面内拉伸不是单纯调节带宽，而是诱导轨道选择性低能重构，大应变下低能态由 Fe-3d z2 主导；摘要明确写出 *"Further strain drives FeTe into an incoherent correlated regime with robust local moments and Fe-3d z2-dominated low-energy states."*

    Magnetic evolution path：磁序路径不是 bicollinear AFM 的线性压制，而是 bicollinear AFM → competing staggered n-mer AFM → stripe AFM；原文为 *"reveal a strain-driven trajectory from bicollinear to stripe antiferromagnetism (AFM) via an intermediate competing staggered n-mer AFM regime in FeTe."*

    Coherence-local moment nonmonotonicity：中等应变产生相干准粒子与弱化局域磁矩共存，大应变恢复强局域磁矩和非相干低能谱；原句 *"Moderate strain gives rise to a regime where more coherent quasiparticles coexist with suppressed local moments."*

    Introduction—

    FeTe as an anomalous parent compound：FeTe 是铁基超导母体材料中极具代表性的体系，特殊性来自块体基态为双共线反铁磁序而非许多铁砷族中的条纹反铁磁序；原文给出清晰对比：*"The magnetic ground state of bulk FeTe is a bicollinear antiferromagnetic (AFM) order rather than the stripe AFM phase common to many other iron pnictides."*

    Hund correlation as the magnetic origin：FeTe 的反常磁性长期被归因于交换相互作用竞争与强 Hund 关联之间的微妙平衡，因而适合研究轨道选择性关联与磁不稳定性耦合；证据句为 *"This unusual magnetic order has long been attributed to the delicate competition between magnetic exchange interactions and strong Hund’s correlations."*

    Experimental motivation from superconducting strained films：最新 FeTe/SrTiO3 薄膜实验显示，化学计量 FeTe 在去除过量间隙 Fe 后可在面内拉伸下超导，说明双共线 AFM 抑制与超导出现紧密相关；原文为 *"stoichiometric FeTe thin films grown on SrTiO3 substrates have been found to become superconducting once excess interstitial Fe is removed."*

    Experimental orbital reconstruction：另一个实验观察到应变诱导轨道重构，核心是 Fe-3d x2-y2 与 Fe-3d z2 之间发生谱重转移；原句 *"strain-induced orbital reconstruction, characterized by a transfer of spectral weight between the Fe-3d x2-y2 and Fe-3d z2 orbitals under tensile strain."*

    Knowledge gap：既有工作已显示中等拉伸可压制 bicollinear AFM 并诱导超导，但完整路径仍缺失，尤其是 bicollinear AFM、staggered n-mer AFM、stripe AFM之间的连续关联及其轨道选择性重构；原文为 *"the complete strain-driven trajectory connecting the competing bicollinear AFM, staggered n-mer AFM, and stripe AFM, together with the accompanying orbital-selective reconstruction, remains incomplete."*

    Research objective：研究目标是建立应变 FeTe 的完整磁相图，并解释轨道重构如何改写 Hund 金属中的磁交换；关键句 *"Addressing this question is crucial for establishing the complete magnetic phase diagram of strained FeTe and for understanding how orbital reconstruction reshapes magnetic interactions in this Hund’s metal."*

    Strain-driven orbital-selective reconstruction of electronic structure—

    Computational setting for electronic structure：电子结构部分聚焦顺磁四方 FeTe，空间群 P4/nmm，系统改变面内晶格常数；原文写明 *"we perform DFT+DMFT calculations on paramagnetic tetragonal FeTe (space group P4/nmm) with systematically varied in-plane lattice constants."*

    DFT baseline metallicity：无关联 DFT 给出多轨道金属图像：Γ 点附近 3 个空穴口袋，M 点附近 2 个电子口袋；证据为 *"The DFT results suggest that FeTe is a multi-orbital metal, with three hole pockets around the Γ point and two electron pockets around the M point."*

    Correlation-induced Hund metal features：纳入 DMFT 后，准粒子带宽明显重整化，谱线展宽，相干性降低，符合 FeTe 为强关联 Hund 金属的共识；原句 *"the quasiparticle bandwidth is significantly renormalized, accompanied by pronounced spectral broadening and suppressed quasiparticle coherence."*

    Two-stage spectral evolution：应变下低能谱呈非单调演化：a=3.81、3.86、3.91、4.01 Å 时费米能级附近谱重和相干性增强；a=4.11、4.22 Å 时低能谱重和准粒子相干性逐步被压制；原文为 *"For the first four in-plane lattice constants (a = 3.81, 3.86, 3.91, and 4.01 Å), ... enhances the spectral weight and coherence"*，以及 *"a = 4.11 and 4.22 Å ... the low-energy spectral weight and the quasiparticle coherence are gradually suppressed."*

    Orbital-selective disappearance of dx2-y2 conduction feature：Γ 点导带底部由 Fe-3d x2-y2 主导；小应变图像中谱重强，但随着拉伸增大逐渐消失，大应变下几乎不可辨认；证据句 *"the bottom of the conduction band at the Γ point that is dominated by the Fe-3d x2-y2 orbital exhibits strong spectral weight for the small-strain cases ... whereas its spectral weight is progressively suppressed ... and eventually becomes barely discernible."*

    Moderate strain induces Fermi-liquid-like crossover：中等拉伸使体系从 FeTe 的非相干区向更接近费米液体的状态交叉；原文为 *"moderate tensile strain enhances quasiparticle coherence, marking a crossover from the incoherent regime of FeTe toward a more Fermi-liquid-like state."*

    Representative stages：a=3.81、4.01、4.22 Å 被选为三个关键阶段，用于分析 Matsubara 自能 ImΣ(iω)、总/轨道谱函数 A(ω) 与 kz=0 费米面；原句 *"we focus on three representative cases (a = 3.81, 4.01, and 4.22 Å), which serve as three key stages along the nonmonotonic evolution."*

    Orbital-dependent self-energy hierarchy at equilibrium：a=3.81 Å 下 Fe-3d 轨道已显示 Hund 金属典型的轨道选择性电子关联；t2g 轨道低频渐近行为比 eg 更尖锐，说明 t2g 关联更强；原文为 *"the Fe-3d orbitals exhibit orbital-selective electronic correlation"* 和 *"the correlation of the Fe-t2g orbitals is stronger."*

    Reduced scattering at moderate strain：a=4.01 Å 时 t2g 轨道 ImΣ(iω) 低频截距更接近零，意味着非相干散射率降低、费米液体倾向增强；证据句 *"the low-frequency intercept of ImΣ(iω) for the Fe-t2g orbitals moves closer to zero, indicating a reduced incoherent scattering rate and an enhanced tendency toward Fermi-liquid behavior."*

    Non-Fermi-liquid large-strain regime：a=4.22 Å 时五个 Fe-3d 轨道 ImΣ(iω) 低频截距都显著增大，非相干电子散射增强，构成非费米液体行为；原文直接表述 *"the low-frequency intercept ... increases markedly for all five Fe-3d orbitals"* 和 *"This is a non-Fermi liquid behavior."*

    dxy as strongest orbital-selective channel：大应变下 Fe-3d xy 轨道低频截距最大，并与其他轨道进一步分离，显示轨道选择性关联增强；原句 *"the Fe-3d xy orbital not only retains the largest low-frequency intercept ... but also becomes increasingly separated from the other orbitals."*

    Spectral-weight transfer to dz2：从 a=3.81 到 4.01 Å，Fe-3d xy 和 Fe-3d z2 低能谱重均显著增加，同时出现从 dxy 到 dz2 的低能谱重再分配，dz2 准粒子峰增强并向费米能级移动；原文为 *"an apparent redistribution of low-energy spectral weight from the Fe-3d xy to the Fe-3d z2 orbital is observed."*

    Large-strain dz2-dominated low-energy structure：a=4.22 Å 时五个 Fe-3d 轨道准粒子谱重均受抑，但 Fe-3d z2 对费米能级附近电子态贡献占主导，标志低能关联电子结构发生根本重构；证据句 *"the Fe-3d z2 orbital provides the dominant contribution to the electronic states near the Fermi level."*

    Fermi-surface reconstruction：小/中等应变下 Γ 点空穴口袋和 M 点电子口袋保持清晰；大应变下费米面相干谱重剧烈丢失，能带拓扑完全重构；原文为 *"Well-defined hole pockets around the Γ point and electron pockets around the M point are preserved under small and moderate tensile strain"*，以及 *"the large-strain regime exhibits a dramatic loss of coherent Fermi-surface spectral weight, as well as a complete reconstruction of band topology."*

    Structural origin through Fe-Te hybridization：轨道重构源于应变改变 Fe-Te 杂化，尤其影响与 Te-5p 强耦合的 Fe-3d xy 态；原句 *"The orbital reconstruction originates from strain-modified Fe-Te hybridization, which selectively affects the Fe-3d xy states strongly coupled with Te-5p orbitals."*

    Quantified local geometry evolution：Fe-Te 键长随拉伸从 a=3.81 Å 时 2.49 Å 增至 a=4.01 Å 的 2.50 Å、a=4.22 Å 的 2.52 Å；面内 Te-Fe-Te 键角从 100.09° 增至 106.82° 和 113.72°；原文为 *"the Fe-Te bond length increases from 2.49 Å ... to 2.50 Å and 2.52 Å"*，以及 *"the in-plane Te-Fe-Te bond angle expands from 100.09° to 106.82° and 113.72°."*

    Strain-driven magnetic transition—

    Electronic localization motivates magnetic analysis：大拉伸下费米面非相干化，说明体系向更局域化关联态演化；在 Hund 金属中，准粒子相干性降低通常伴随稳定局域磁矩形成；证据句 *"the correlated electronic state of the system gradually evolves toward a more localized regime"* 和 *"the suppression of quasiparticle coherence is generally accompanied by the formation of robust local magnetic moments."*

    Static local spin susceptibility trend：χspin 在 a=3.81 到 4.01 Å 范围内温度依赖较弱；100 K 对比显示 χspin 随拉伸非单调变化：从 a=3.81 到 3.91 Å 略降，随后在更大应变下逐渐升高；原文为 *"the χspin exhibits a weak temperature dependence"*，以及 *"showing a slight decrease from a = 3.81 to 3.91 Å, followed by a gradual increase under larger strains."*

    Unified coherence-moment picture：中等拉伸抑制局域磁矩形成并稳定更相干的关联电子结构；更大拉伸推动体系进入局域磁矩主导区，并显示 Curie-Weiss-like 局域磁化率温度依赖；原句 *"moderate tensile strain suppresses local-moment formation and stabilizes a more coherent correlated electronic structure"*，以及 *"larger strain drives the system toward a local-moment-dominated regime characterized by a Curie-Weiss-like temperature dependence."*

    Definition of effective local moment：局域自旋构型用 Mz = sqrt(<Sz2>) 定量表征，由局域自旋多重态计算；大 Mz 表示统计权重集中到高自旋局域多重态，局域磁矩更稳定；原文为 *"we introduce an effective local spin moment Mz = sqrt(<Sz2>) to quantitatively characterize the evolution of local spin configurations"* 和 *"a large Mz indicates that the statistical weight is increasingly concentrated on high-spin local multiplets."*

    Local multiplet evidence：Table 2 给出 100 K 下 Fe-3d 局域多重态权重。N=6、Sz=2 权重从 a=3.81 Å 的 12.23% 增至 a=4.01 Å 的 14.02%、a=4.11 Å 的 17.43%；N=7、Sz=1.5 权重从 17.25% 增至 19.01% 和 21.73%。这支撑高自旋组分增强，与原句 *"Mz exhibits a gradual increase under small and moderate tensile strains, followed by a much sharper enhancement in the large-strain regime."* 一致。

    DFT energy hierarchy at equilibrium：a=3.81 Å 时 bicollinear AFM 为能量零点，即基态；相对能量为 NM 186.33 meV/f.u.、FM 84.52、Néel 71.49、stripe 21.17、Dimer 4.37、Trimer 9.10。原文说明 *"the bicollinear AFM order is found to be the ground state, in agreement with numerous previous studies."*

    Intermediate n-mer competition：a=3.86、3.91、4.01 Å 时 Dimer AFM 均为最低能态，Trimer AFM 与其接近：Trimer 相对能量分别为 4.15、3.35、0.54 meV/f.u.；stripe 相对能量分别为 17.37、16.06、7.39 meV/f.u.，bicollinear 则升至 6.16、14.63、32.43 meV/f.u.。原文概括为 *"the energy difference between the Dimer and Trimer AFM orders is progressively reduced"* 和 *"indicative of an increasingly competing magnetic landscape."*

    Superconductivity-relevant strain window：中等应变区同时具有 Dimer/Trimer 近简并、强磁竞争、准粒子相干增强，并与近期化学计量 FeTe 薄膜超导报道的应变窗口重合；证据句 *"this specific intermediate strain range ... coincides with the regime in which superconductivity has been recently reported."*

    Stripe stabilization at large strain：a=4.11 Å 与 4.22 Å 时 stripe AFM 为最低能态；a=4.11 Å 下相对能量为 NM 243.96、Néel 68.07、bicollinear 51.12、Dimer 4.84、Trimer 2.14 meV/f.u.；a=4.22 Å 下为 NM 301.01、Néel 84.65、bicollinear 77.32、Dimer 19.86、Trimer 13.74 meV/f.u.。FM 在 a=4.11/4.22 Å 自洽过程中不稳定并弛豫到 NM；表注为 *"the ferromagnetic (FM) configuration is unstable and relaxes to a nonmagnetic (NM) solution."*

    Heisenberg phase criteria：DFT 总能映射到 J1-J2-J3 Heisenberg 模型。bicollinear AFM 条件为 J3 > J2/2 且 J2 > J1/2；stripe AFM 条件为 J2 > J1/2 且 J3 < J2/2；staggered n-mer 低于 stripe 的条件为 J1 - 2J2 + 2J3 > 0。原文给出 *"The bicollinear AFM phase is energetically stabilized when J3 > J2/2 and J2 > J1/2"*。

    Trajectory in exchange-coupling phase diagram：提取的交换相互作用不仅符合既有 J1-J2-J3 相图，还显示清晰应变轨迹：a=3.81 Å 位于 bicollinear 区，a=3.86-4.01 Å 位于 staggered n-mer 区且靠近 bicollinear-stripe 边界，进一步拉伸进入 stripe 区；证据为 *"reveal a well-defined strain-driven evolution trajectory on the phase diagram."*

    Final microscopic synthesis：FeTe 作为 Hund 金属，在平衡结构中由 Hund 耦合稳定强局域自旋矩与 bicollinear AFM；中等应变进入 staggered n-mer 区，Dimer/Trimer 近简并，局域磁矩被削弱，低能准粒子相干性提高；大应变恢复强局域磁矩，跨入 stripe AFM，并伴随 Fe-3d z2 主导低能态。原文压缩句为 *"FeTe, as a typical Hund’s metal, hosts bicollinear AFM order with robust local spin moments stabilized by Hund’s coupling."*

    Discussion and Conclusion—

    Comprehensive physical picture：应变如何同时改变 FeTe 的磁性与关联电子态此前并不完整；现在形成了轨道选择性电子结构重构与磁相演化耦合图像；原句 *"how lattice strain modifies the magnetism and the associated correlated electronic states of FeTe remains incomplete"* 和 *"revealing the intimate interplay between orbital-selective electronic structure reconstruction and magnetic phase evolution."*

    Intermediate regime as key superconductivity context：中间区的标志不是磁性简单消失，而是增强磁竞争与相干准粒子共存，并与化学计量 FeTe 薄膜超导应变窗口重叠；证据句 *"an intermediate regime characterized by enhanced magnetic competition and coherent quasiparticles"* 和 *"overlaps with the strain window where superconductivity has been reported."*

    Strain engineering implication：应变工程可用于操纵 FeTe 的关联量子相；单轴应变、压力和局域晶格调制也可能提供新调控路径；原文为 *"strain engineering as a route for manipulating correlated quantum phases in FeTe"* 和 *"uniaxial strain or pressure, may also provide opportunities."*

    Contrast with cuprates：与空穴掺杂铜氧化物不同，拉伸 FeTe 的超导相关中间区不是由 Mott 能隙瓦解定义，而是由竞争 AFM 相和相干电子态定义；原文为 *"the intermediate regime of tensile-strained FeTe is not characterized by the destruction of a Mott gap, but rather by the competing AFM phases with coherent electronic states."*

    Common thread across unconventional superconductors：尽管电子环境不同，强磁涨落可能是非常规超导体系之间的共同机制线索；证据句 *"strong magnetic fluctuations stand out as a common thread linking these seemingly distinct unconventional superconducting systems."*

    Final conclusion：FeTe 是研究 Hund 关联、非费米液体行为和磁性耦合的平台；面内双轴拉伸驱动磁基态从 bicollinear AFM 经 competing staggered n-mer orders 到 stripe AFM，并同步诱导轨道选择性重构；原句 *"In-plane biaxial tensile strain drives the magnetic ground state from the bicollinear AFM phase to the stripe AFM phase through an intermediate regime of competing staggered n-mer orders."*

    Note added—

    Concurrent pairing study：手稿准备期间出现另一项工作，研究电子掺杂化学计量 FeTe 的超导配对；原句为 *"During the preparation of this manuscript, we noticed another work that studied the superconducting pairing of electron-doped stoichiometric FeTe."* 这意味着当前结果与配对机制研究存在潜在互补关系，但并未直接计算配对对称性或 Tc。

    Acknowledgments—

    Funding and computing support：资助来源包括国家自然科学基金 12434009、国家重点研发计划 2024YFA1408601、2024YFA1408602、2022YFA1403103，以及量子科学技术创新计划 2021ZD0302402；计算资源来自中国人民大学高性能计算物理实验室。原文为 *"Computational resources were provided by the Physical Laboratory of High Performance Computing in Renmin University of China."*

    Appendix A End Matter / Method of calculations—

    Structure optimization protocol：不同应变块体 FeTe 的晶体结构使用 QUANTUM ESPRESSO 优化，只固定面内晶格常数；原文为 *"The crystal structures of different strained bulk FeTe were optimized by using the QUANTUM-ESPRESSO package, in which only the in-plane lattice constants were fixed."*

    Magnetic DFT setup：磁性用 VASP 计算；NM、FM、Néel AFM、stripe AFM、bicollinear AFM、Dimer AFM 使用 sqrt(2) × sqrt(2) 超胞，Trimer AFM 使用 3sqrt(2) × 3sqrt(2) 超胞；原文为 *"The Trimer AFM state was studied with a 3sqrt(2) × 3sqrt(2) supercell."*

    Exchange-correlation functional：所有 DFT 计算采用 PBE 型 GGA；原句 *"The generalized gradient approximation of Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof type for the exchange-correlation potentials was adopted in all DFT calculations."*

    DFT+DMFT implementation：DFT+DMFT 中 DFT 部分用 WIEN2K 的全势线性缀加平面波方法，DMFT 用 EDMFTF，并执行电荷完全自洽；原文为 *"The EDMFTF software package is used to perform the charge fully self-consistent DFT+DMFT calculations."*

    Paramagnetic correlated subspace：体系强制为顺磁；Fe-3d 轨道作为关联轨道；顺磁晶胞中两个 Fe 等价，因此只构造一个杂质问题；原句 *"The systems are enforced to be paramagnetic"* 和 *"a single impurity problem was constructed because the two Fe atoms are equivalent within the paramagnetic unit cell."*

    Local orbital convention：Fe 局域坐标中 x、y 轴沿 Fe-Fe 键方向，z 轴平行全局晶体 z 轴；Fe-3d 轨道按该局域坐标定义。这一点决定了 dxy、dx2-y2、dz2 等轨道标签的物理含义；原文为 *"The local coordinate system of Fe atoms was defined with the x and y axes aligned along the Fe-Fe bond directions."*

    Interaction parameters：库仑相互作用 U=5.0 eV，Hund 交换 JH=0.88 eV，为 3d 过渡元素典型值；采用密度-密度形式库仑排斥；原句为 *"The Coulomb interaction parameter U was set to be 5.0 eV, and the Hund’s exchange parameter JH was set to be 0.88 eV."*

    Impurity solver and continuation：有限温 DMFT 杂质问题用 hybridization expansion CTQMC 求解；自能采用精确双计数；实频自能由最大熵解析延拓获得，用于动量分辨谱函数等物理量；证据句 *"The real-frequency self-energy function was obtained by using the maximum entropy method analytical continuation."*

    Heisenberg mapping：Heisenberg 模型包含最近邻 J1、次近邻 J2、次次近邻 J3，哈密顿量为 H=J1Σ<ij>Si·Sj + J2Σ<<ij>>Si·Sj + J3Σ<<<ij>>>Si·Sj；原文为 *"we considered the nearest, the next-nearest, and the next-next-nearest neighbor couplings J1, J2, and J3."*

    Energy-to-exchange extraction：假设不同磁序的能量差主要来自 Fe 自旋矩之间相互作用，用 Néel、stripe、bicollinear、Dimer AFM 的能量求解 J1、J2、J3；原句 *"The effective exchange parameters J1, J2, and J3 are calculated based on the energy of Néel, stripe, bicollinear, and Dimer AFM states."*

    Local multiplet statistics：Table 2 在 100 K 给出不同应变下 Fe-3d 局域自旋多重态权重，量子数为总占据 N 与总自旋投影 Sz。例如 a=4.11 Å 时 N=6、Sz=2 权重 17.43%，N=7、Sz=1.5 权重 21.73%，高自旋态增强；表头说明 *"The good quantum numbers N and Sz denote the total occupancy and total spin of the Fe-3d orbitals."*

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持

    1. 术语解析

    Orbital-selective reconstruction：不是所有轨道同步变化，而是不同 Fe-3d 轨道在低能谱重、相干性和费米面贡献上发生差异化重排。这里的关键差异是 dx2-y2 谱重减弱、dxy 控制相干性、dz2 接管费米能级附近低能态。英文 *"orbital-selective"* 强调选择性作用于轨道自由度，不等同于普通能带移动。

    Hund’s metal：指强关联主要由 Hund 交换 JH驱动的金属态，而非典型 Mott 机制。其特征包括轨道选择性关联、较大局域磁矩、相干-非相干交叉和非费米液体散射。FeTe 中 U=5.0 eV、JH=0.88 eV 的设置正是为了描述这种 3d 多轨道 Hund 物理。

    Quasiparticle coherence：指低能电子能否以寿命较长、能带清晰的准粒子形式存在。谱函数中表现为费米能级附近峰更尖锐、谱重更集中；自能中表现为 ImΣ(iω) 低频截距更接近零。它不是“导电性一定增强”的同义词，而是多体散射减弱的低能判据。

    Low-frequency intercept of ImΣ(iω)：Matsubara 自能低频外推截距可作为非相干散射强弱的指标。截距接近零通常对应费米液体式低能相干；截距较大说明有限散射率和非费米液体倾向。这里 a=4.01 Å 的 t2g 截距靠近零，而 a=4.22 Å 五个 3d 轨道截距显著增大。

    Staggered n-mer AFM：一种由若干相邻 Fe 自旋组成 n-mer 单元并交错排列的反铁磁状态，包含 Dimer、Trimer 等竞争构型。它不是单一传统 Néel 或 stripe 序，而是处在 bicollinear 与 stripe 之间的竞争磁区；判据中 J1 - 2J2 + 2J3 > 0 时其能量可低于 stripe。

    2. 疑难查证

    为什么中等拉伸会增强相干性，而更大拉伸反而增强局域磁矩？

    中等拉伸改变 Fe-Te 键长和键角，使某些轨道的杂化与能级位置更有利于形成清晰低能准粒子，表现为 Fe-3d xy 与 dz2 低能谱重增强、t2g 自能低频截距下降。继续拉伸后，Fe-Te 杂化被进一步重排，dxy 对低能相干结构的贡献削弱，dz2 变成低能主导轨道，但整体散射增强，五个 3d 轨道谱重下降；此时电子更局域，局域磁矩恢复并增强。

    为什么 bicollinear AFM 不只是被压制，而会走向 stripe AFM？

    磁序由 J1、J2、J3 的相对大小决定。bicollinear 需要较强 J3，即 J3 > J2/2；stripe 在 J2 > J1/2 且 J3 < J2/2 时占优。拉伸改变 Fe-Te-Fe 几何与 Fe-Te 杂化，进而改写交换通道，使相图轨迹从 bicollinear 区穿过 staggered n-mer 竞争区后进入 stripe 区。因此“磁性被压制”只描述了中间窗口，不能描述全应变路径。

    为什么超导相关窗口被解释为强磁竞争而非简单弱磁性？

    中等应变下 Dimer 与 Trimer 能量差缩小到 meV/f.u. 量级，例如 a=4.01 Å 时 Trimer 仅比 Dimer 高 0.54 meV/f.u.，stripe 也降至 7.39 meV/f.u.；同时局域磁矩被削弱、准粒子相干性增强。这样的状态具有可传播的低能电子和强烈竞争磁涨落，天然适合作为非常规配对的候选环境。

    为什么 dz2 主导低能态重要？

    铁基超导通常强调 dxz/dyz、dxy 等 t2g 轨道对低能物理的重要性。大应变下 dz2 主导费米能级附近态意味着低能轨道成分发生定性改变，会改变费米面拓扑、轨道选择性散射、交换路径和可能的配对通道。因此这不是普通谱重变化，而是低能有效模型本身的重写。

    第五部分：创新评估与关联

    创新性判断

    From isolated observations to unified strain phase trajectory：过去 2-5 年内的关键实验已经分别观察到化学计量 FeTe 薄膜超导、磁序可逆调控、应变诱导轨道相关性变化，但缺少一条把电子相干性、轨道重构、局域磁矩和磁基态切换贯通起来的理论路径。该研究的新颖性在于给出完整序列：bicollinear AFM → staggered n-mer competing regime → stripe AFM，并把该序列投影到 J1-J2-J3 相图。

    Identification of the intermediate superconductivity-relevant regime：新结果强调中等拉伸区不是普通非磁金属，而是 Dimer/Trimer 近简并 + 磁竞争增强 + 准粒子相干增强 + 局域磁矩削弱的复合态。这个判断比“拉伸压制 bicollinear AFM 后出现超导”更具体，因为它指出了可能促进非常规配对的能量尺度与电子态条件。

    Orbital-selective mechanism beyond magnetic energetics：既有磁性相图研究多聚焦总能量或交换参数；这里同时解析 ImΣ(iω)、A(ω)、费米面、Fe-Te 键长/键角、局域多重态，把磁交换变化追溯到应变调控的 Fe-Te 杂化与轨道选择性谱重转移。尤其是从 dxy/dx2-y2 相关特征向 dz2 主导低能态的演化，是对 FeTe 应变物理的新微观约束。

    Resolution of the apparent contradiction between coherence and magnetism：FeTe 中应变既可增强电子相干性，又可在更大应变下恢复强局域磁矩，这看似矛盾。非单调路径解释了这一点：中等应变靠近费米液体式相干区，大应变进入非费米液体和局域矩区。该框架避免把应变效应误解为单调“增强金属性”或单调“压制磁性”。

    Remaining unresolved problems：尚未直接计算超导配对相互作用、能隙对称性、Tc 或界面 SrTiO3 声子/电荷转移影响；DFT+DMFT 参数如 U=5.0 eV、JH=0.88 eV 采用典型值，参数敏感性未展开；大应变 a=4.22 Å 对真实薄膜可达性、结构稳定性和缺陷影响仍需实验验证。下一步最关键的是把该相图与 ARPES、RIXS/INS、自旋涨落谱和薄膜可控应变实验直接对接。

  5. 05
    高熵过渡金属碳化物中的晶界预熔化#
    Grain-Boundary Premelting in High-Entropy Transition Metal Carbides#
    📊 含图深析
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Marium M. Mou, Caleb Schenck, Samuel E. Daigle, William G. Fahrenholtz, Bharat Gwalani, Stefano Curtarolo, Donald W. Brenner🔥 AI 100
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：利用 Monte Carlo（MC）采样和分子动力学（MD）模拟，并结合通用的 MACE-OMAT-0 机器学习原子间势，对四种高熵过渡金属碳化物中的晶界偏聚和热致界面无序化进行了研究。MC 采样使第 VI 族元素（Cr、Mo 或 W）和 Zr 偏聚到晶界，在那里第 VI 族元素含量达到约 45 at.%，这与 STEM-EDS 观察结果一致。在 MD 升温过程中，晶界的 Lindemann 指数，即原子间距离涨落的归一化度量，在 Cr 含碳化物中于约 1390ⁱ̧rcC、Mo 含体系中于 1660ⁱ̧rcC、W 含体系中于 1890ⁱ̧rcC 附近达到液体样阈值 δ=0.15，而晶内仍低于该阈值。一个化学随机的（Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr）C 参考体系则晚约 60ⁱ̧rcC 才跨过阈值，并表现出较弱的边界局域无序，凸显了界面化学在预熔化中的作用。按物种分辨的位移显示晶界迁移增强，尤其是碳原子。总体而言，富 Cr 的界面最早且最广泛地表现出类似预熔化的响应，其次是含 Mo 和含 W 的晶界。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27273v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Grain-boundary segregation and thermally induced interfacial disordering were investigated in four high-entropy transition metal carbides using Monte Carlo (MC) sampling and molecular dynamics (MD) with the universal MACE-OMAT-0 machine-learning interatomic potential. MC sampling segregated the group-VI element (Cr, Mo, or W) and Zr to grain boundaries, where the group-VI content reached approximately 45 at.%, consistent with STEM-EDS observations. During MD heating, the grain-boundary Lindemann index, a normalized measure of interatomic distance fluctuations, reached the liquid-like threshold of δ=0.15 near 1390ⁱ̧rcC for the Cr-containing carbides, 1660ⁱ̧rcC for Mo, and 1890ⁱ̧rcC for W, while the grain interiors remained below the threshold. A chemically random (Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C reference crossed about 60ⁱ̧rcC later and showed less boundary-localized disorder, highlighting the role of interfacial chemistry in premelting. Species-resolved displacements showed enhanced grain-boundary mobility, particularly for carbon. Overall, Cr-rich interfaces showed the earliest and most extensive premelting-like response, followed by Mo- and W-containing boundaries.

    💡 亮点：研究将蒙特卡洛化学采样与 MACE-OMAT-0 分子动力学结合，解析四种高熵过渡金属碳化物的晶界偏析和预熔化。Cr、Mo 或 W 以及 Zr 在晶界的第 VI 族元素含量约达到 45 at.%，并与 STEM-EDS 观测一致。升温过程用晶界 Lindemann 指数刻画原子距离无序化，建立成分偏析与热稳定性的联系。该工作对团队的机器学习势、晶界、缺陷和高温结构动力学具有直接参考价值。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：论文以蒙特卡洛采样处理四种高熵过渡金属碳化物的晶界化学偏析，再采用结合MACE-OMAT-0机器学习势的分子动力学追踪升温无序化。摘要给出Cr、Mo或W等第VI族元素及Zr在晶界富集、局部含量约45 at.%的结果，并以Lindemann指数识别晶界预熔化，且称与STEM-EDS一致。具体四种成分、晶界五自由度、势函数微调方式、训练数据、MC系综、MD时间尺度、升温速率和预熔化温度没有给出。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该研究对应高熵合金/陶瓷的偏析热力学、晶界复相、Lindemann熔化判据和ML势驱动的长时间大体系MD。与常规DFT晶界计算相比，MC加MACE可同时探索多元占位和有限温度动力学，但势函数的域外可靠性仍是关键；与UF³相比，MACE是等变深度模型，通常更擅长复杂局域化学。题干没有团队五位成员的既有工作，不能虚构具体关联；可连接其若有的MACE训练、缺陷热力学、STEM表征和铁电畴壁研究。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将流程迁移到BaTiO3或NbOI2的晶界、畴壁和掺杂界面：先用DFT构造不同终止、氧空位/卤素空位、应变及掺杂占位训练数据，微调MACE或与NEP并行验证。半巨正则MC用于获得温度和化学势下的偏析谱，随后在NPT或受控应力MD中计算Lindemann指数、局域配位、极化分布和离子扩散。应以STEM-EDS/EELS偏析、原位加热衍射或PFM畴壁响应核验，并用独立DFT抽样和主动学习补充高温罕见构型。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题高熵过渡金属碳化物中，晶界偏析是否会在低于体相熔点的加工温度下诱发 premelting-like 无序化；这种失稳是否不仅取决于是否偏析，还取决于偏析元素种类、晶界局域化学和单碳化物耐火性层级。
    创新方法用通用机器学习势 MACE-OMAT-0 结合 Monte Carlo 金属原子交换和分级升温 MD，在四类高熵碳化物、五个模型中同时比较晶界化学偏析与热无序化；关键亮点是加入 Cr-rich 与化学随机对照，直接把‘局域界面化学’与‘premelting 起点’对应起来。
    工作流程先用 Voronoi 构建三晶粒多晶超胞并做弛豫；再用 MC 仅交换金属亚晶格、保持碳亚晶格固定，得到晶界偏析结构；随后做两类升温 MD，一类统计 MSD 和液态样位移趋势，另一类精细扫描晶界无序化起点；最后用 Lindemann 指数、liquid-like pair fraction 和原子位移分析晶界/晶内差异，并与 STEM-EDS 对照。
    关键结果Cr、Mo、W 与 Zr 都明显向晶界富集，其中 group-VI 元素晶界含量约 44.6–46.6 at.%；晶界无序化起点呈清晰层级，Cr-rich 最早，约 1390–1450 °C，Mo 约 1660 °C，W 约 1890 °C；1900 °C 时 Cr-rich 晶界液态样对比例最高，fₗᵢq,GB 约 0.51–0.52，且晶界 Lindemann 指数显著高于晶内。
    应用价值说明高熵碳化物晶界设计不能只看偏析强度，还必须看偏析元素本身的耐火性和局域结构；这为烧结致密化、晶界强度调控、裂纹沿晶/穿晶转变以及高温服役稳定性提供了可计算的设计依据。

    第一部分：核心概览 (Overview)

    高熵过渡金属碳化物的晶界失稳不只是由“是否偏析”决定，而由偏析元素的化学身份、晶界局域成分、单金属碳化物耐火性层级共同控制。基于 MACE-OMAT-0 通用机器学习势、MC 金属原子交换和 MD 升温模拟，四类 HETMC 中 Cr/Mo/W 与 Zr 均向晶界富集，group-VI 元素晶界含量约 44.6–46.6 at.%。Cr 富集晶界最早发生 premelting-like disordering，Lindemann 阈值 δ = 0.15 在约 1390–1430 °C 被跨越，Mo 为 1660 °C，W 为 1890 °C。该工作把实验观察到的 Cr/W 晶界偏析与原子级热扰动、碳迁移和单碳化物熔化趋势连接起来，为高熵碳化物烧结、致密化和晶界力学性能差异提供了可计算解释框架。

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《Grain-Boundary Premelting in High-Entropy Transition Metal Carbides》（None，）

    背景：HETMC 研究重点从相稳定性和体相性质转向加工依赖微结构、晶界偏析与力学行为。Cr、Mo、W 虽同属 group-VI，但碳化物熔点与热力学性质差异显著，可能导致不同晶界 premelting 行为。

    方法：四种组分、五个模型：MC-segregated `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`、MC-segregated `(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`、chemically random `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`、MC-segregated `(Hf,Mo,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`、MC-segregated `(Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C`。使用 MACE-OMAT-0 small potential，MC 进行 5 × 10⁵ 次金属原子交换，MD 升温计算 Lindemann index、liquid-like pair fraction、MSD，并与 SEM/STEM-EDS 晶界偏析观察对照。

    结果：group-VI 元素在晶界均强烈富集：Cr 45.6/46.3 at.%，Mo 46.6 at.%，W 44.6 at.%；Zr 也富集至 25.9–35.3 at.%。晶界 premelting 阈值顺序为 Cr-rich 最早、Mo 居中、W 最晚：约 1390、1430、1660、1890 °C。1900 °C 时 Cr-rich 晶界 fₗᵢq,GB ≈ 0.51–0.52，W-rich 为 0.35。

    讨论：Cr 晶界更易失稳与 Cr-C 系较低熔点、Cr 富集界面化学、碳亚晶格高迁移率共同相关。随机 Cr 模型比 MC-segregated 模型晚约 60 °C 跨越阈值，说明晶界局域化学有独立影响。

    局限：MD 升温速率远高于实验；Lindemann 阈值 δ = 0.15 只是操作性 disorder 指标；MC 固定总元素数量，不能模拟实验中液相流出导致的成分损失；碳亚晶格固定进行 MC，未纳入碳空位对偏析和扩散的影响；单相升温熔化指标不是平衡熔点。

    结论：HETMC 晶界稳定性存在明确化学层级：Cr-rich interfaces showed the earliest and most extensive premelting-like response, followed by Mo- and W-containing boundaries。晶界设计不能只看偏析强度，还必须看偏析相的耐火性、局域结构和界面处碳/金属迁移。

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析 (Section-by-Section Condensing)

    Abstract

    Segregation-disordering coupling：四种 HETMC 中同时考察了晶界偏析和热诱导界面无序化，方法组合为 MC sampling、MD 与 MACE-OMAT-0 universal machine-learning interatomic potential。证据原句为 *"Grain-boundary segregation and thermally induced interfacial disordering were investigated in four high-entropy transition metal carbides using Monte Carlo (MC) sampling and molecular dynamics (MD) with the universal MACE-OMAT-0 machine-learning interatomic potential."*

    Group-VI and Zr enrichment：MC 显示 Cr/Mo/W 与 Zr 均向晶界偏析，group-VI 元素晶界含量约 45 at.%，并与 STEM-EDS 一致。关键证据为 *"MC sampling segregated the group-VI element (Cr, Mo, or W) and Zr to grain boundaries, where the group-VI content reached approximately 45 at.%, consistent with STEM–EDS observations."*

    Premelting hierarchy：Lindemann index 使用 δ = 0.15 作为 liquid-like threshold；Cr-containing 晶界约 1390 °C 达阈值，Mo 约 1660 °C，W 约 1890 °C，晶粒内部低于阈值。原文证据为 *"reached the liquid-like threshold of δ = 0.15 near 1390 °C for the Cr-containing carbides, 1660 °C for Mo, and 1890 °C for W, while the grain interiors remained below the threshold."*

    Interfacial chemistry effect：chemically random `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 比 MC-segregated 模型约晚 60 °C 跨阈值，且 disorder 更少局限于晶界。证据为 *"A chemically random (Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C reference crossed about 60 °C later and showed less boundary-localized disorder, highlighting the role of interfacial chemistry in premelting."*

    Carbon mobility dominance：物种分辨位移显示晶界原子迁移增强，尤其是 carbon。原句为 *"Species-resolved displacements showed enhanced grain-boundary mobility, particularly for carbon."*

    I Introduction

    Field shift from bulk descriptors to grain boundaries：早期 HETMC 研究集中于相稳定性、entropy-forming ability、valence electron concentration，随后焦点转向processing-dependent microstructures、GBs、mechanical behavior。原文表述为 *"Early studies of high-entropy transition metal carbides (HETMCs) emphasized phase stability and bulk property predictions using descriptors such as entropy-forming ability and valence electron concentration."* 以及 *"attention has increasingly shifted toward processing-dependent microstructures, grain boundaries (GBs), and their effects on mechanical behavior."*

    Cr as an unusual group-VI element：Cr 在 group-VI 元素中位置特殊，已有实验观察到 Cr 晶界偏析及其力学影响。原文为 *"Among the group-VI transition metals (Cr,Mo,W), chromium occupies a unique position."*，并进一步指出 *"Several recent experimental studies have reported Cr segregation to GBs in HETMCs and the effect of this segregation on mechanical properties."*

    Prior Cr-Hf-Nb-Ta-Zr carbide evidence：Wang 等对 spark plasma sintering 制备的 `(Cr,Hf,Nb,Ta,Zr)C` 发现晶内 Cr 溶解度约 2.5 at.%、GB Cr 富集、更高密度；该组成由 DEED 预测为多相。证据为 *"They report limited Cr solubility of about 2.5 at.% in the grains, Cr enrichment at the GBs, and a higher density"*，并说明 *"This composition is predicted to be multi-phase based on the Disordered Enthalpy–Entropy Descriptor (DEED)."*

    Cr-Nb-Ta-Ti-Zr solubility and second phase：Su 等在 1800–2000 °C pressureless sintering 下制备 `(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`，DEED = 20，接近单相/多相阈值 19；Cr 溶解极限约 3.8 at.%，GB 偏析，并在 Cr 升高时形成 Cr3C2 triple junctions。原句包括 *"The DEED value of 20 for this composition predicts a single-phase, but the value is near the single-multiphase threshold of 19."* 与 *"They report a Cr solubility limit of ∼3.8 at.%, Cr segregation to GBs, and formation of Cr3C2 at triple junctions as the Cr concentration is increased."*

    Hot pressing and liquid Cr carbide hypothesis：后续 hot-pressing at 2100 °C 未见第二相，Cr 添加降低孔隙率，被归因于液态 Cr carbide 在材料内外流动；断裂从 intergranular 转向 transgranular，暗示 GB strength 增强。证据为 *"They report no second phases and a decrease in porosity with Cr addition."*、*"They attribute this to flow of liquid Cr carbide inside and out of the material during processing."*、*"They also report a transition from inter- to trans-granular fracture with increasing Cr content, suggesting an increase in the GB strength."*

    Cr uniqueness remains unresolved：Cr 行为是否独特尚不清楚；Cr/Mo/W 的碳化物热力学和熔点差异大，晶界稳定性可能强烈依赖偏析物种。关键原文为 *"Whether this behavior is unique to Cr remains unclear."* 和 *"Group-VI transition metals exhibit markedly different carbide thermodynamics and melting temperatures, suggesting that the stability of a chemically segregated GB may depend strongly on the identity of the segregating species."*

    Cr vs W experimental contrast：Schenck 等研究 `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C`，由金属氧化物与碳 carbothermal reduction 合成粉末，在 2000 °C、50 MPa direct current sintering 致密化；DEED = 18，预测多相；晶内 Cr <5 at.%，GB 强偏析。证据为 *"densified in mild vacuum using direct current sintering at 2000 °C and a peak uniaxial pressure of 50 MPa"*，以及 *"they report <5 at.% Cr concentration in the grains and strong Cr segregation to the GBs."*

    W retains more bulk content and less interfacial enrichment：W 替代 Cr 的 `(Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C` 也发生 W 向 GB 偏析，但晶内 W 约 11.2 at.%，高于 Cr 体系的 <5 at.%，界面 W 浓度低于 Cr 对应界面浓度。原文为 *"the W-containing composition retained a larger bulk concentration of W, approximately 11.2 at.%, compared with less than 5 at.% Cr"*，并且 *"exhibited a noticeably lower interfacial concentration of W than the corresponding interfacial concentration of Cr."*

    Fracture behavior separates segregation from stability：尽管 Cr 和 W 都偏析，W 体系表现为 transgranular fracture 和更高 compressive strength；Cr 体系表现为 intergranular fracture、较低 compressive strength 和 indentation pop-in events。证据为 *"The W-containing composition showed transgranular fracture and higher compressive strength, whereas the Cr-containing composition showed intergranular fracture, comparatively lower compressive strength, and pop-in events during indentation."* 这支持结论：*"segregation alone does not determine the GB response"*。

    Carbide melting temperatures motivate premelting question：CrCx 熔点约 1550–1810 °C，WC 高于 2700 °C；在 sintering temperature 下 CrCx 可能为液态，WC 仍为固态。原文为 *"the melting temperature of CrCx ranges from approximately 1550–1810 °C, depending on stoichiometry, whereas WC melts above 2700 °C."* 以及 *"CrCx is likely to be liquid at the sintering temperature, while WC is expected to remain solid."*

    Three explicit research questions：核心问题为：Cr 界面浓度是否足以在加工温度以下诱导 premelting；界面结构独立于总体成分的作用；MoCx 熔点居于 CrCx 与 WCx 之间时是否也会出现低于加工温度的 interfacial premelting。原文直接列出 *"i. Is the concentration of interfacial Cr sufficient to induce premelting below the processing temperature? ii. What role does the interfacial structure play in premelting, independent of overall composition? iii. MoCx has a melting temperature at substoichiometric compositions that lies between those of CrCx and WCx: is MoCx therefore predicted to exhibit interfacial premelting below the processing temperature?"*

    MLIP enables cross-composition comparison：OMat24 等通用 MLIP 使复杂多组分陶瓷在无需 composition-specific fitting 下可模拟；MACE 具备跨化学空间可迁移精度，适合统一条件比较多个 HETMC GB。证据为 *"Recent universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) trained on broad inorganic-materials databases, such as OMat24, provide a practical framework for simulating complex multicomponent ceramics without composition-specific fitting."* 与 *"This makes it practical to compare GB behavior across multiple HETMC compositions under identical simulation conditions"*。

    Five-model simulation design：模拟覆盖四种 HETMC、五个模型：两种 Cr-containing、两种 Cr-free，加上 `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 的 MC-segregated 与 chemically random 对照。原文为 *"we carried out Monte Carlo (MC) and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations using the pretrained universal MACE-OMAT-0 MLIP across five models representing four HETMC compositions."* 并列出 *"The Cr-containing compositions are (Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C and (Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C, while the Cr-free compositions are (Hf,Mo,Ta,Ti,Zr)C and (Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C."*

    II Materials and Methods

    II.1 Experimental

    Specimen synthesis route：Cr 与 W 两类样品沿用既有制备路线：等摩尔金属氧化物粉末与 carbon black 混合，高能球磨 1 h，在 1610 °C、3 h、mild vacuum 下 carbothermal reduction。原文为 *"nominally equimolar amounts of metal-oxide powders were combined with carbon black"*，*"mixed by high energy ball milling for 1 h"*，以及 *"underwent carbothermally reduction at 1610 °C for 3 h under mild vacuum."*

    Densification conditions：carbide powders 经 direct current sintering 10 min，单轴压力 50 MPa；Cr 体系致密化温度 2000 °C，W 体系 1950 °C。证据为 *"densified by direct current sintering for 10 min under a uniaxial pressure of 50 MPa"* 和 *"The densification temperatures were 2000 °C for (Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C and 1950 °C for (Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C."*

    Microscopy and spectroscopy setup：SEM 使用 Hitachi SU3900 backscatter detector，操作电压 20 kV；化学分析为 SEM in-situ EDS；TEM lamellae 由 FIB lift-out 制备；BF-TEM 与 STEM-EDS 在 200 kV 下使用 Thermo Fisher Titan 80–300 和 Talos F200X。原文为 *"Polished sections were examined using a backscatter detector in a scanning electron microscopy (SEM; Hitachi SU3900) operated at 20 kV."* 以及 *"BF-TEM and STEM–EDS were performed at 200 kV using Thermo Fisher Titan 80–300 and Talos F200X microscopes."*

    Composition profiling geometry：成分线扫垂直于选定晶界提取，用于判断 GB enrichment 的局域性。原句为 *"Composition profiles were extracted normal to selected GBs."*

    II.2 Computational

    Polycrystal geometry and size：所有四种组分由 Atomsk Voronoi tessellation 生成；初始 rock-salt carbide slab 为 53.0 × 53.0 × 4.5 Å³，三晶粒 seed 坐标为 (9.4,14.6,2.3)、(39.5,15.6,2.3)、(23.9,40.6,2.3) Å，面内取向为 −178.9°、−136.7°、−33.5°；初始 1062 atoms 沿 z 复制 11 次，得到 53.0 × 53.0 × 49.5 Å³、11,682 atoms 周期超胞。证据为 *"a 53.0 × 53.0 × 4.5 Å3 rocksalt carbide slab was partitioned into three grains"* 与 *"producing a periodic 53.0 × 53.0 × 49.5 Å3 supercell containing 11,682 atoms."*

    Potential choice and validation rationale：所有 MC 和 MD 使用未经额外训练或微调的 pretrained MACE-OMAT-0 small potential。选择原因是相对 DFT 成本低，且此前在 universal MLIPs 中与 DFT 总体一致性最好，可再现 Σ5(210) carbide GB energies 和 entropy-forming ability 趋势。原文为 *"performed using the pretrained MACE-OMAT-0 small potential without additional training or fine-tuning"*，以及 *"showed the best overall agreement with DFT among the universal MLIPs tested in our previous study."*

    Initial relaxation protocol：每个结构在 LAMMPS 中先 conjugate-gradient minimization，然后 100 fs NVT 和 500 fs NPT 于 27 °C 平衡；两个阶段 timestep 都为 0.05 fs，以稳定处理 Voronoi 构建和化学随机化后的局部高力。证据为 *"followed by 100 fs of NVT equilibration and 500 fs of NPT equilibration at 27 °C"* 与 *"Both equilibration stages used a 0.05 fs timestep to maintain stable integration"*。

    Monte Carlo metal-sublattice sampling：MC 只交换金属原子，carbon sublattice fixed，不考虑 carbon-vacancy effects；每次随机选两个不同金属身份交换，MACE 直接评估 trial energy，在 300 K 按 Metropolis criterion 接受；每个计算扩展至 5 × 10⁵ attempted swaps，直到 potential energy plateau。原文为 *"chemical ordering was sampled by MC atom swaps while keeping the carbon sublattice fixed"*，*"excludes carbon-vacancy effects on segregation"*，以及 *"each calculation was extended to 5 × 10⁵ attempted swaps."*

    Broad-range annealing for MSD：第一类 NPT heating 用于 species-resolved MSD：约 27 °C 平衡 1.0 ps，从 27 到 1127 °C 用 2.2 ps，再到 2727 °C 用 16.0 ps，总轨迹 19.2 ps。证据为 *"This trajectory was used for the species-resolved mean-squared-displacement (MSD) analysis."*

    Focused heating for disorder onset：第二类慢速升温用于更准确定义 GB disordering onset：先约 2.0 ps 平衡，然后从 1127 到 1927 °C 分四个 200 °C 区间，以 25 °C ps−1 升温；每段 8.0 ps，总分析加热数据约 32 ps。原文为 *"followed by heating from 1127 to 1927 °C in four consecutive 200 °C intervals at a constant rate of 25 °C ps−1"* 与 *"yielding approximately 32 ps of analyzed heating data."*

    Lindemann index definition：premelting-like disorder 由局域 pair-relative Lindemann index δ_R 定量，温度区间为 50 °C；包括 C–C、C–metal、metal–metal pairs；pair-resolved δᵢⱼ = 0.15 作为 substantial local disorder 的操作阈值。原文为 *"Premelting-like interfacial disorder was quantified ... using a local pair-relative Lindemann index, δR"*，*"C–C, C–metal, and metal–metal pairs were included"*，以及 *"A threshold of δij = 0.15 was used as an operational indicator of substantial local disorder."*

    Liquid-like pair fraction caveat：fₗᵢq,R 定义为区域中 δᵢⱼ ≥ 0.15 的局域 pair 比例，但不解释为热力学 liquid volume fraction。原文强调 *"This quantity represents the fraction of locally disordered pairs and was not interpreted as a thermodynamic liquid volume fraction."*

    Threshold interpretation caution：Lindemann 临界值依赖材料、键合环境和 δ 定义，因此不作为完全液态证据，而通过 GB 与 grain interior 的相对响应判定 onset。证据为 *"the threshold was not treated as absolute evidence of a fully liquid state"* 和 *"disordering onset was identified by comparing the GB region and grain-interior responses."*

    MSD population control：MSD 针对 C 和相关偏析金属 Cr、Zr、Mo、W、Nb；GB 原子选取距离 reference boundary 2.5 Å 内，grain-interior 原子选每个 species 最远 30%；每个 species 与 composition 使用 equal-sized GB/interior populations 减少采样偏差。原文为 *"GB atoms were selected within 2.5 Å of the reference boundary"*，*"grain-interior atoms were selected from the farthest 30% of each species"*，以及 *"Equal-sized GB and grain-interior populations were used separately for each species and composition to minimize sampling bias."*

    Single carbide melting simulations：单金属碳化物熔化行为由 one-phase NPT heating 评估：rocksalt 初始结构，零外压 isotropic relaxation，约 27 °C 初始化，连续升至 4227 °C，速率 10 °C ps−1；420 ps ramp、4.2 × 10⁵ MD steps、timestep 1.0 fs；enthalpy、volume、density、MSD 每 1 ps 采样，构型每 5 ps 保存。原句为 *"continuously heated to 4227 °C at 10 °C ps−1"* 与 *"The 420 ps ramp comprised 4.2 × 10⁵ MD steps using a 1.0 fs timestep."*

    Melting indicator not equilibrium melting point：过渡温度由 enthalpy、volume、MSD 同时增加识别；因单晶连续升温可能 superheating，结果解释为 one-phase heating melting indicators，不是 equilibrium melting points。原文为 *"Transition temperatures were identified from simultaneous increases in enthalpy, volume, and MSD"*，并说明 *"these temperatures were interpreted as one-phase heating melting indicators rather than equilibrium melting points, since crystalline superheating may occur."*

    III Results and discussion

    III.1 Experimental observations of grain-boundary segregation

    Cr-rich boundary signal：STEM-EDS line-scan 显示 `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 选定 GB 附近存在 localized Cr atomic fraction increase，其他元素较均匀，说明 Cr enrichment 可发生于单个 GB，而非只存在于更大 Cr-rich 微结构。原文为 *"the Cr elemental map shows a Cr-rich region associated with the analyzed boundary"*，*"EDS profile exhibits a distinct, localized increase in the Cr atomic fraction near the position at which the scan crosses the GB"*，以及 *"not restricted to larger Cr-rich features observed elsewhere in the microstructure."*

    W-rich boundary signal weaker and broader：`(Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C` 中 W elemental map 也显示 GB 附近局域 W-rich 区，line profile 有 modest but distinct W 增加；相比 Cr peak，W enrichment 更弱且分布距离更宽。证据为 *"The corresponding line profile exhibits a modest but distinct increase in the W atomic fraction near the GB position."* 与 *"Compared with the pronounced Cr peak, the W enrichment in this selected region is weaker and distributed over a somewhat broader distance."*

    Experimental support for simulation trend：Cr 和 W 都具有向 interfacial regions 偏析的定性证据。原句为 *"These measurements qualitatively support the simulated tendency of both Cr and W to segregate toward interfacial regions."*

    Previously observed secondary features：既有显微结果显示 Cr-rich regions 位于 GBs 和 triple junctions；W-rich platelets 或 nanograins 沿部分 GB network 分布；高分辨 STEM 和衍射识别为 W-rich phase，并在部分区域观察到 carbon depletion。原文为 *"Additional microscopy reported previously showed Cr-rich regions at GBs and triple junctions, as well as W-rich platelets or nanograins along selected portions of the grain-boundary network."* 以及 *"identified the latter features as a W-rich phase, with local carbon depletion observed in some regions."*

    III.2 Computational grain-boundary segregation and experimental comparison

    All compositions develop chemical heterogeneity：最终 MC-sampled structures 在四种 HETMC 中均显示明显化学非均匀性。原文为 *"The final MC-sampled structures reveal pronounced chemical heterogeneity across all four polycrystalline HETMC compositions."*

    Group-VI enrichment is strongest：每种组分中 group-VI 元素都是最强 GB-enriched species：Cr 在 `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 为 45.6 at.%、在 `(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 为 46.3 at.%，enrichment ratios 2.28、2.31；Mo 为 46.6 at.%、ratio 2.33；W 为 44.6 at.%、ratio 2.23。原文为 *"In each composition, the group-VI element is the most strongly enriched GB species."* 并给出 *"Cr reaches 45.6 at.% ... and 46.3 at.% ... corresponding to enrichment ratios of 2.28 and 2.31"*，*"Mo reaches 46.6 at.%"*，*"W reaches 44.6 at.%"*。

    Zr co-segregates in all systems：Zr 在四个体系中也富集，GB concentration 25.9–35.3 at.%。证据为 *"Zr is also enriched in all four systems, with GB concentrations ranging from 25.9 to 35.3 at.%"*。

    Element-specific depletion pattern：Ta 和 Hf 在 GB 强烈贫化，Ti 不同程度贫化，Nb-containing 体系中 Nb 接近中性。原句为 *"In contrast, Ta and Hf are strongly depleted at the GBs, Ti is depleted to varying degrees, and Nb remains nearly neutral in the Nb-containing composition."*

    Quantitative table-level segregation details：`(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 中 GB: Cr 45.6、Zr 35.3、Hf 5.6、Ta 8.6、Ti 4.8 at.%；interior: Cr 12.8、Zr 15.7、Hf 24.1、Ta 23.2、Ti 24.3 at.%。`(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 中 GB: Cr 46.3、Zr 25.9、Nb 19.5、Ta 5.6、Ti 2.7 at.%；interior: Cr 13.0、Zr 18.4、Nb 20.1、Ta 23.8、Ti 24.6 at.%。这些数据对应表题 *"Species-resolved grain-boundary segregation in four HETMCs."*

    Cr-free quantitative segregation details：`(Hf,Mo,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 中 GB: Mo 46.6、Zr 33.3、Ti 9.5、Ta 5.9、Hf 4.8 at.%；interior: Mo 12.0、Zr 16.0、Ti 23.1、Ta 24.2、Hf 24.6 at.%。`(Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C` 中 GB: W 44.6、Zr 32.3、Ti 13.1、Hf 5.7、Ta 4.3 at.%；interior: W 12.8、Zr 16.4、Ti 22.0、Hf 24.2、Ta 24.6 at.%。表中说明 *"cGB and cint denote the GB and grain-interior compositions, respectively, and cGB/cref is the enrichment ratio relative to cref = 20.0 at.%"*。

    Agreement and caveats versus experiment：Cr 强富集与 STEM-EDS 观察一致；但 300 K MC 得到的相似 Cr/W 晶界浓度与实验中不同 Cr/W bulk/interfacial concentration 可能因加工效应而不同。关键原文为 *"The strong Cr enrichment is consistent with experimental STEM–EDS observations"*，以及 *"the different Cr and W concentrations observed experimentally likely reflect additional processing-dependent effects."*

    Two modeling caveats：模拟中各 species 总数固定，而实验中元素可通过流动等方式损失；碳空位作用未探索。原文为 *"the number of each species is kept constant while they can be lost through flow or other means in the experiment"* 和 *"we have not explored the possible role of carbon vacancies."*

    III.3 Composition-dependent grain-boundary disordering

    MC-generated heterogeneous GBs require thermal stability testing：MC 产生化学非均匀 GB，随后通过 focused heating simulations 评估热稳定性。原句为 *"Because MC sampling generates chemically heterogeneous GB regions, their thermal stability was evaluated using the focused heating simulations."*

    Clear hierarchy of interfacial disordering：Cr-rich 晶界最早失稳，MC-segregated `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 在 T_cross ≈ 1390 °C 达到 δ = 0.15；`(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 约 1430 °C；chemically random `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 约 1450 °C。原文为 *"A clear hierarchy of interfacial disordering is observed."*，并列出 *"reaches the adopted threshold at Tcross ≈ 1390 °C, followed by ... approximately 1430 °C and chemically random ... approximately 1450 °C."*

    Chemical ordering advances disorder onset：MC-segregated 结构中 Cr-only projections 显示 Cr 沿 GB network 明显积累，random configuration 中 Cr 更均匀；Lindemann 响应显示 MC-segregated 结构更早、更局域的 disordering。原文为 *"pronounced Cr accumulation along the GB network in the MC-segregated structure"*，*"the random configuration retains a more uniform Cr distribution"*，以及 *"confirm earlier and more strongly localized disordering in the MC-segregated configuration."*

    Boundary-specific excess disorder quantifies localization：MC-segregated 结构的 δ_GB − δᵢₙₜ 从约 0.040 增至 0.056；random configuration 保持在约 0.031–0.038。原文为 *"The boundary-specific excess disorder, δGB − δint, increases from approximately 0.040 to 0.056 for the MC-segregated structure, whereas the random configuration remains between approximately 0.031 and 0.038."*

    Cr-rich chemistry rather than Cr alone：MC 交换所有金属，因此更早 premelting 归因于整体 Cr-rich interfacial chemistry，而非单独 Cr 原子存在。原句为 *"Because MC sampling redistributes all metal species, this behavior is attributed to the resulting Cr-rich interfacial chemistry rather than to Cr alone."*

    Mo and W delayed disordering：Cr-free 模型明显推迟 GB disordering：`(Hf,Mo,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 在约 1660 °C 达 δ = 0.15；`(Hf,Ta,Ti,W,Zr)C` 到约 1890 °C 才达阈值。原文为 *"The two Cr-free models exhibit substantially delayed GB disordering."*，并给出 *"near 1660 °C"* 与 *"approximately 1890 °C"*。

    Disordering remains interfacial：所有五个模型中，grain-interior Lindemann indices 在分析温区内都低于 0.15，说明 substantial disorder 优先发生在 interfaces，而非晶内均匀出现。证据为 *"In all five models, the grain-interior Lindemann indices remain below 0.15 throughout the analyzed temperature range"*。

    Heating-rate limitation：MD 升温速率远快于实验，温度数值应作为比较性 simulation metrics；更慢升温或实验可能移动 onset。原文为 *"Because the MD heating rate is much faster than experimental heating rates, these temperatures should be viewed as comparative simulation metrics; slower heating or experiments may shift the observed onset."*

    III.4 Liquid-like pair fraction and species-resolved atomic mobility

    Liquid-like pair fraction measures disorder breadth：fₗᵢq = fδᵢⱼ≥₀.₁₅ 衡量局域 atomic pairs 中超过 Lindemann threshold 的比例，反映强局域 disorder 在区域内分布多广。原文为 *"This metric represents the fraction of local atomic pairs whose pair-resolved displacement fluctuations exceed the Lindemann threshold and therefore indicates how broadly strong local disordering is distributed within each region."*

    1900 °C table metrics establish Cr-rich dominance：1900 °C 附近，MC-segregated Cr-rich GB 的 fₗᵢq,GB 最大：`(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 为 0.51，`(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 为 0.52；random Cr 模型 0.47；Mo 0.41；W 0.35。原文为 *"the MC-segregated ... boundaries exhibit the largest liquid-like pair fractions, with fliq,GB ≈ 0.52"*，并指出 *"more than half of the local neighbor pairs within these Cr-rich GB regions exceed the adopted disordering threshold."*

    1900 °C Lindemann values and ratios：表 2 中 1900 °C 指标为：MC-segregated `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` δ_GB 0.176、δᵢₙₜ 0.120、δ_GB/δᵢₙₜ 1.47；MC-segregated `(Cr,Nb,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 0.178、0.121、1.47；random `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 0.167、0.130、1.28；Mo 模型 0.160、0.113、1.42；W 模型 0.150、0.112、1.34。表题原文为 *"Comparison of GB and grain-interior disordering across the five HETMC models."*

    Spatial localization strongest in MC-segregated Cr models：两种 MC-segregated Cr-containing 模型在 1900 °C 的 δ_GB/δᵢₙₜ ≈ 1.47，表示 GB disorder 约比晶内高 50%。原文为 *"the two MC-segregated Cr-containing models exhibit ratios of approximately 1.47, indicating nearly 50% greater disorder at the GB than in the grain interior."*

    Random Cr model has weakest localization contrast：chemically random `(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 的 ratio 最小 1.28，原因是 grain-interior Lindemann index 较高、削弱 GB/interior contrast。证据为 *"the chemically random ... model has the smallest ratio, 1.28, because its higher grain-interior Lindemann index reduces the contrast between the two regions."*

    MSD identifies mobility trends rather than exact thresholds：MSD broad-range annealing 使用不同温区和升温速率，因此只用于比较相对 mobility，不用于确定 threshold-crossing temperatures。原文为 *"the MSD results are used to identify relative mobility trends rather than to determine threshold-crossing temperatures."*

    GB populations generally more mobile：相关元素的 GB populations 通常比 grain-interior populations 位移更大；enhanced GB MSD 是 premelting-like behavior 的互补证据，表明 disordered interfacial region 中 mobility 增强。原句为 *"For the relevant constituent species, the GB populations generally exhibit larger displacements than the corresponding grain-interior populations."* 以及 *"The enhanced GB MSD provides complementary evidence for premelting-like behavior"*。

    Carbon is the dominant mobile species：C 的 mobility response 最强；GB C 位移显著增加，而 grain-interior C 大体局域。证据为 *"Carbon shows the strongest mobility response"* 和 *"C atoms in the GB undergo a marked increase in displacement, whereas grain-interior C atoms remain largely localized."*

    Carbon mobility mechanism：这种行为与 transition-metal carbides 中 vacancy-mediated carbon transport 一致，碳扩散可显著快于 metal-sublattice diffusion；虽未引入 C vacancies，GB 局部 undercoordination 也可能促进 C mobility。原文为 *"This behavior is consistent with vacancy-mediated carbon transport in transition-metal carbides, which can be substantially faster than metal-sublattice diffusion."* 以及 *"local undercoordination associated with the polycrystalline GB structure may also contribute to the enhanced interfacial C mobility."*

    Metal rearrangement follows carbon disorder：Cr 和 Zr 绝对 MSD 较低，但高温下逐渐变得 mobile，暗示早期 carbon-sublattice disorder 后发生 metal-species rearrangement。证据为 *"Cr and Zr show lower absolute MSD values but become increasingly mobile at elevated temperature"*，以及 *"early carbon-sublattice disorder is followed by metal-species rearrangement within the chemically heterogeneous GB region."*

    Cr mobility links to processing observations：增强的 Cr mobility 与 HETMC 高温加工中 Cr-assisted densification 和 diffusion 报道一致。原句为 *"The enhanced Cr mobility is also consistent with reports of Cr-assisted densification and diffusion during high-temperature processing of HETMCs."*

    III.5 Single metal carbide melting-point trends and implications for grain-boundary stability

    GB disordering follows carbide melting hierarchy：晶界 disordering 顺序大体跟随对应单金属碳化物熔点层级。原文为 *"The GB disordering sequence broadly follows the melting-point hierarchy of the corresponding single metal carbides."*

    CrC one-phase transition benchmark：CrC 中 enthalpy、volume 同时增加并伴随 MSD 快速增长，定义 transition region 2003–2103 °C；transition score 给出 one-phase heating melting indicator 2052 °C。证据为 *"Coincident increases in enthalpy and volume, together with the onset of rapid MSD growth, define a transition region from approximately 2003 to 2103 °C."* 以及 *"giving a one-phase heating melting indicator of 2052 °C."*

    Predicted single-carbide melting indicators：MACE-OMAT-0 预测：B1 CrC 2052 °C，B1 MoC 2603 °C，B1 WC 2717 °C，TiC 3288 °C，NbC 3653 °C，ZrC 3752 °C，HfC 4085 °C，TaC 4111 °C。原文为 *"The predicted melting temperatures are 2052 °C for B1 CrC, 2603 °C for B1 MoC, 2717 °C for B1 WC, 3288 °C for TiC, 3653 °C for NbC, 3752 °C for ZrC, 4085 °C for HfC, and 4111 °C for TaC."*

    Transition signatures differ by carbide：表 3 中 CrC transition interval 2003–2103 °C，ΔH 0.195 eV/atom，ΔV 3.14%，MSD slope before/after 0.643/2.965 Å² ps−1；MoC 为 2533–2663 °C、ΔH 0.307、ΔV 6.66%、MSD slope 0.649/4.668；WC 为 2663–2773 °C、ΔH 0.255、ΔV 6.50%、MSD slope 0.422/3.396。表题说明 *"MACE-OMAT-0-predicted single metal carbide one-phase heating melting indicators."*

    High-refractory end members remain much hotter：TiC、NbC、ZrC、HfC、TaC 的 one-phase melting indicators 均显著高于 group-VI carbide lower end：TiC 3288 °C，NbC 3653 °C，ZrC 3752 °C，HfC 4085 °C，TaC 4111 °C。原句为 *"preserve the overall experimental ranking, from the comparatively low melting temperature of the Cr–C system to the highly refractory HfC and TaC end members."*

    Simulation ranking supports chemistry-dependent GB stability：CrC < MoC < WC 的低温端排序与 Cr-rich、Mo-rich、W-rich GB premelting onset 的 1390/1660/1890 °C 顺序一致，说明局域偏析相耐火性可作为晶界热稳定性的一级预测因子。支撑原文为 *"These values broadly track the experimental melting-point trend."* 与 *"the stability of a chemically segregated GB may depend strongly on the identity of the segregating species."*

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持 (Understanding & Nuance)

    Premelting / interfacial premelting：不是“已经形成宏观液相”，而是晶界在低于体相熔点时出现类似液体的局域无序、原子距离涨落和迁移增强。这里用 premelting-like 保持谨慎，因为 δ = 0.15 不是绝对液态证明。关键语境为 *"the threshold was not treated as absolute evidence of a fully liquid state"*。学术微妙点：premelting 强调界面先于晶内失稳；interfacial disordering 更中性，避免声称真正熔化。

    Lindemann index：衡量原子间距随时间涨落相对于平均间距的无量纲指标。δ 越大，局域结构越不稳定。这里采用 pair-relative 形式，包含 C–C、C–metal、metal–metal pairs，并以 δᵢⱼ ≥ 0.15 作为 local disorder 判据。微妙点：它是结构动力学指标，不等价于热力学相判据。

    Liquid-like pair fraction：指局域 pair 中超过 Lindemann 阈值的比例，反映无序化覆盖范围，而非液体体积分数。原文明确 *"was not interpreted as a thermodynamic liquid volume fraction"*。因此 fₗᵢq,GB = 0.52 应理解为“超过一半邻近 pair 呈强涨落”，而不是“52% 晶界已成液体”。

    MC-segregated vs chemically random：chemically random 是元素随机分布的对照；MC-segregated 是通过 Metropolis 交换在 300 K 采样到低能化学重排后的结构。两者总体成分相同，但局域晶界化学不同。约 60 °C onset 差异说明 premelting 不是整体成分平均效应，而是晶界局域化学效应。

    One-phase heating melting indicator：连续升温单晶结构得到的转变温度，会受 superheating 影响，因此不是平衡熔点。它的价值在于同一 MLIP、同一 protocol 下比较 CrC/MoC/WC/TiC 等相对排序。语境证据为 *"rather than equilibrium melting points, since crystalline superheating may occur."*

    疑难概念：为什么 Cr 偏析可能削弱晶界，而 W 偏析不一定？

    偏析只说明某元素喜欢待在晶界，不自动说明晶界稳定或脆弱。Cr 和 W 都能富集到 GB，但 Cr-C 体系熔点低，Cr-rich interfacial chemistry 在加工温度附近更容易产生局部无序、碳迁移和金属重排；W-C 更耐火，因此即使 W 富集，界面仍更晚达到 Lindemann disorder 阈值。换句话说，晶界性能由三层因素叠加决定：第一，元素是否偏析；第二，偏析后形成的局域化学环境是否接近低熔点/低稳定性组成；第三，界面结构是否提供 undercoordination、free volume 或碳迁移路径。

    疑难概念：为什么碳先动不等于整块材料熔化？

    过渡金属碳化物由金属亚晶格和碳亚晶格共同构成。碳原子小、可通过空位或欠配位区域迁移，扩散通常比金属快。GB 中局部结构松散，C–metal 配位不完整，C 的 MSD 可显著升高，但金属骨架仍可能保留一定有序性。因此早期 premelting-like response 更像“界面碳亚晶格先软化/活化”，随后 Cr/Zr 等金属在高温下逐渐重排。

    第五部分：创新评估与关联 (Synthesis & Novelty)

    创新性判断

    From segregation observation to thermal-disordering mechanism：过去 2–5 年相关工作已经观察到 Cr segregation、W segregation、Cr-assisted densification、fracture mode changes，但主要停留在显微表征和加工-性能相关性层面。该研究把晶界偏析推进到原子级热稳定性问题：偏析元素不只改变成分，还改变 GB premelting onset、disorder localization 和 atomic mobility。

    Cr/Mo/W systematic comparison under one protocol：过去对 Cr-rich HETMC 的讨论较多，W 体系也有实验对比，但 Mo 作为位于 CrCx 与 WCx 熔点之间的中间参照较少被系统纳入。统一使用四种组分、五个模型、同一 MACE-OMAT-0 势函数和相同 MC/MD protocol，建立了 Cr earliest → Mo intermediate → W latest 的晶界稳定性层级。

    Interfacial chemistry isolated by random-vs-segregated control：`(Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C` 的 chemically random 与 MC-segregated 对照是关键创新点。总体成分相同，但 MC-induced Cr-rich interface 使 T_cross 降低约 60 °C、δ_GB − δᵢₙₜ 更高，证明 premelting 对局域界面化学敏感，而非单纯平均成分或晶界几何效应。

    Universal MLIP applied to complex ceramics without composition-specific fitting：高熵碳化物化学空间复杂，DFT 难以覆盖多晶、大尺寸、长温程；传统势函数难以跨 Cr/Mo/W/Hf/Ta/Ti/Zr/Nb/C。MACE-OMAT-0 使 11,682 atoms 三晶粒模型、5 × 10⁵ MC swaps 和多温区 MD 成为可行，展示了通用 MLIP 在 refractory ceramics GB 研究中的方法学价值。

    Linking single-carbide refractory ranking to GB premelting：单金属碳化物 one-phase melting indicators 提供解释框架：CrC 2052 °C、MoC 2603 °C、WC 2717 °C 的相对顺序，与 HETMC GB disordering onset 1390/1660/1890 °C 相匹配。创新点不在于精确预测平衡熔点，而在于把局域偏析化学与可比较的耐火性指标关联起来。

    Unresolved but important next questions：后续最需要补强的是含 carbon vacancies 的 MC/MD、不同 GB character 和更大 polycrystal ensembles、慢速升温或增强采样对 T_cross 的影响、真实烧结中 Cr-rich liquid flow 导致的成分损失，以及 premelting 指标与断裂韧性、GB cohesion、压痕 pop-in 的定量连接。当前证据已经足以支持化学层级判断，但还不足以给出实验加工窗口下的绝对 premelting 温度。

    阅读原文 ↗
