    双层 kagome 铁磁体 Co3Sn 中的本征反常霍尔响应#
    Intrinsic anomalous Hall response in the bilayer kagome ferromagnet Co₃Sn#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Yuqi Qin, Soumya Sankar, Xingkai Cheng, Yifan Jiang, Shiming Lei, Junwei Liu, Berthold Jäck🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：过渡金属kagome磁体为研究层间堆叠、磁有序和能带拓扑之间的相互作用提供了丰富平台。在此，我们报道了kagome金属Co₃Sn高质量薄膜的分子束外延生长和实验研究；该材料此前尚未以块体形式合成。结构和化学分析证实，该材料具有六方晶格结构（P6₃/mmc），由直接A-B堆叠的Co₃Sn kagome双层组成。磁学测量显示出稳定的面内易轴铁磁性，其居里温度超过300 K。磁输运测量表明，该材料具有金属性，其载流子浓度约为5.01×10²² cm⁻³；从2 K一直到室温，其反常霍尔电导率均近似与温度无关，约为90 Ω⁻¹·cm⁻¹。第一性原理密度泛函理论计算结果将这一反常霍尔响应归因于自旋分裂能带中费米能级附近的本征贝里曲率热点。我们的结果确立了Co₃Sn是一种室温kagome铁磁体，并突出了层堆叠序列对CoSn家族kagome金属材料性质的影响。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27025v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Transition-metal kagome magnets provide a rich platform for investigating the interplay between layer stacking, magnetic order, and band topology. Here, we report the molecular beam epitaxy and experimental investigation of high-quality thin films of the kagome metal Co₃Sn, which has not been synthesized in bulk form yet. Structural and chemical analyses confirm a hexagonal lattice structure (P6₃/mmc) composed of direct A-B stacked Co₃Sn kagome bilayers. Magnetometry reveals robust easy-plane ferromagnetism with a Curie temperature exceeding 300,K. Magneto-transport measurements demonstrate metallic behavior (carrier density napprox5.01×10²²,cm⁻³) alongside a temperature-independent anomalous Hall conductivity of σₘ̊ AHEapprox90,Ømega⁻¹ḑotm̊ cm⁻¹, extending from 2,K up to room temperature. Results from first-principles density functional theory calculations attribute this anomalous Hall response to intrinsic Berry curvature hotspots near the Fermi level in the spin-split band structure. Our results establish Co₃Sn as a room-temperature kagome ferromagnet and highlight the impact of the layer stacking sequence on the material properties of kagome metals from the CoSn family.

    💡 亮点：Co3Sn 薄膜实现了直接 A-B 堆垛 kagome 双层结构，并显示居里温度超过 300 K 的易面铁磁性。工作把不可获得块体样品的 Co3Sn 推进到可测薄膜平台，重点考察拓扑能带与反常霍尔响应。它与团队的 kagome 磁体、Wannier 输运和自旋轨道耦合研究直接相关，但摘要未给出 Berry 曲率或实验霍尔电导。

    多铁半导体 GeMnTe 中激光诱导 Rashba 自旋轨道力矩#
    Laser-Induced Rashba Spin-Orbit Torques in Multiferroic Semiconductor (Ge,Mn)Te#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zeynab Sadeghi, Tomas Ostatnicky, Eva Schmoranzerova, Jozef Kimak, Dominik Kriegner, Helena Reichlova, Lukas Nadvornik, Gunther Sprinhgholtz, J. Hugo Dil, Juraj Krempasky, Petr Nemec🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：多铁性半导体GeMnTe同时具有铁电性、强拉什巴自旋轨道耦合以及载流子介导的磁有序，使其成为探索电子、结构和磁自由度相互作用的独特平台。在本研究中，我们利用时间分辨磁光谱学研究了Ge0.85Mn0.15Te的超快磁化动力学。通过在瞬态响应中分离磁性和非磁性贡献，我们识别出两种不同的激光诱导磁现象，而二者均源于光生有效自旋轨道转矩。相干磁化进动源于激光诱导的空穴浓度增加；这一变化改变了拉什巴分裂且自旋锁定的价带态的占据，并由此改变了磁易轴。通过矫顽场变化观测到的瞬态磁有序变化，被归因于激光诱导的铁电亚晶格位移变化；这些变化影响铁电极化以及与之相关的拉什巴自旋轨道相互作用。第一类光学自旋轨道转矩此前已在稀磁半导体（Ga，Mn）As中被观测到，而第二种效应是多铁性拉什巴半导体（Ge，Mn）Te所特有的，为在自旋轨道电子学器件中实现磁有序的全光操控和自旋轨道转矩产生开辟了新的机会。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26364v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The multiferroic semiconductor GeMnTe exhibits ferroelectricity, strong Rashba spin-orbit coupling, and carrier-mediated magnetic order, making it a unique platform for exploring the interplay among electronic, structural, and magnetic degrees of freedom. In this study, we investigate the ultrafast magnetization dynamics of Ge0.85Mn0.15Te using time-resolved magneto-optical spectroscopy. By separating magnetic and nonmagnetic contributions in the transient response, we identify two distinct laser-induced magnetic phenomena, both resulting from photoinduced effective spin-orbit torque. Coherent magnetization precession arises from a laser-induced increase in hole concentration, which modifies the occupation of Rashba-split spin-locked valence band states and alters the magnetic easy axis. The transient change in magnetic ordering, observed as variations in the coercive field, is attributed to laser-induced modifications of the ferroelectric sublattice displacement, which affect the ferroelectric polarization and the associated Rashba spin-orbit interaction. While the first type of optical spin-orbit torque has already been observed in the diluted magnetic semiconductor (Ga,Mn)As, the second effect is unique to the multiferroic Rashba semiconductor (Ge,Mn)Te, opening new opportunities for all-optical manipulation of magnetic order and spin-orbit torque generation in spin-orbitronic devices.

    💡 亮点：Ge0.85Mn0.15Te 把铁电极化、Rashba 自旋轨道耦合和载流子介导磁性结合到同一半导体中。时间分辨磁光谱通过分离磁性与非磁性信号识别出两类激光诱导磁响应。该体系直接对应团队的多铁、自旋轨道耦合和超快自旋动力学方向，但摘要截断使两种机制、时间尺度和定量幅度无法确认。

    有限温度下 d 波交替磁体中的涌现 Skyrmion 霍尔效应#
    Emergent Skyrmion Hall Effect in d-wave Altermagnets at Finite Temperature#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Tingting Liu, Bingyu Sun, Fengyue Zhu, Zhihui Zhang, Peiyu Zhang, Yang Liu, Minghui Qin🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：反铁磁体将补偿性的磁有序与非常规的、依赖对称性的响应结合起来，为自旋电子学应用提供了一个很有前景的平台。本文表明，电压控制的磁各向异性梯度在缺乏热涨落时驱动 d 波反铁磁（ATM）斯格明子以近乎直线、无霍尔偏转的方式运动，这归因于其强补偿的陀螺动力学响应。热磁振子通过增强磁振子-斯格明子散射导致纵向拖曳增大，并通过依赖手性的偏斜散射产生横向反作用力，从而从定性上改变这一行为。由于反铁磁磁振子带结构具有各向异性，两种手性的磁振子在沿 x 和 y 方向传播时的相对输运权重会互换，导致横向斯格明子漂移的符号相反。相比之下，沿高对称方向时，两种磁振子手性保持简并，其横向贡献相互抵消，即使在有限温度下也能保持无霍尔偏转运动。因此，我们揭示了一种热诱导的各向异性斯格明子霍尔效应，其方向相关的大小与符号源于内禀的、对称性依赖的磁振子谱，使其成为 ATM 斯格明子在有限温度下的一种普适动力学特征。我们的结果为实现电控、热可调的 ATM 斯格明子输运建立了一条低功耗路线。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27107v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated magnetic order with unconventional symmetry-dependent responses, offering a promising platform for spintronic applications. Here, we show that a voltage-controlled magnetic-anisotropy gradient drives altermagnetic (ATM) skyrmions in a nearly rectilinear, Hall-free manner in the absence of thermal fluctuations, owing to their strongly compensated gyrotropic response. Thermal magnons qualitatively modify this behavior by increasing the longitudinal drag through magnon--skyrmion scattering and generating a transverse reaction force through handedness-dependent skew scattering. Owing to the anisotropic altermagnetic magnon band structure, the relative transport weights of the two magnon handednesses are interchanged between propagation along the x and y directions, resulting in transverse skyrmion drifts of opposite sign. By contrast, along the high-symmetry direction, the two magnon handednesses remain degenerate and their transverse contributions cancel, preserving Hall-free motion even at finite temperature. We thus uncover a thermally emergent anisotropic skyrmion Hall effect whose direction-dependent magnitude and sign originate from the intrinsic symmetry-dependent magnon spectrum, making it a generic finite-temperature dynamical feature of ATM skyrmions. Our results establish a low-power route toward electrically controlled and thermally tunable ATM skyrmion transport.

    💡 亮点：d 波交替磁体中的 Skyrmion 在无热涨落时因陀螺响应强补偿而近似无霍尔运动。有限温度引入热磁振子散射，增加纵向拖曳并恢复横向响应。工作把交替磁拓扑纹理从静态对称性推进到温度依赖输运，直接对应团队的自旋哈密顿量、蒙特卡洛和自旋动力学方向，但摘要未给出温度、速度或霍尔角数值。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：与团队研究高度相关，涉及交替磁性、磁各向异性、拓扑磁态、热涨落和自旋动力学。Hongjun Xiang 的有效自旋哈密顿量和蒙特卡洛可提供温度依赖磁参数，Hongyu Yu 的自旋晶格耦合模型可检验声子是否改变梯度驱动；团队也关注 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用和器件自旋功能。本文具体 d 波模型与候选材料尚未在摘要中明确，不能直接指定材料映射。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”补充训练/验证集，再比较《有限温度下 d 波交替磁体中的涌现 Skyrmion 霍尔效应》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

Kamba (Institute of Physics, Czech Academy of Sciences, Prague, Czech Republic)🔥 AI 98
    相关度

    📄 摘要：当前开发新型数据操控方式的研究，旨在实现磁性材料中磁化强度的超快控制，并在抗磁性材料中诱导磁矩。我们证明，在抗磁性量子顺电体KTaO₃中，振幅约为300 kV/cm的圆偏振太赫兹脉冲电场能够通过共振激发其简并软极性声子，诱导瞬态类磁响应。我们利用太赫兹泵浦-光学探测技术，通过时间分辨磁光法拉第效应测量了这种声子介导的响应。我们的探测方案被设置为抵消电光克尔效应的主要部分，因为该效应通常也会对瞬态响应作出显著贡献。随后，通过相减与相反圆偏振太赫兹辐射对应的实验数据，进一步抑制了与克尔效应相关的信号。因此，我们能够明确识别出KTaO₃晶体中具有温度依赖性的类磁行为，该行为表现为提取出的法拉第旋转。我们建立了一个理论模型，能够对测得的瞬态法拉第效应信号曲线进行良好的定量描述。然而，这些曲线的幅度呈现出出乎意料的温度依赖性，这可能是深入理解所观察到的声子磁效应的关键。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27060v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The current efforts striving to develop new ways of data manipulation are aimed at ultrafast control of magnetization in magnetic materials, as well as at inducing magnetic moments in diamagnetics. We demonstrate that in the diamagnetic quantum paraelectric KTaO₃, the electric field of circularly polarized THz pulses with an amplitude of sim 300,kV/cm induces a transient magnetic-like response by resonantly exciting its degenerate soft polar phonon. This phonon-mediated response was measured using the THz pump---optical probe technique via the time-resolved magneto-optic Faraday effect. Our detection scheme was set up to cancel out the major part of the electro-optic Kerr effect which also usually significantly contributes to the transient response. The Kerr-effect-related signal was further suppressed by subtracting the experimental data related to oppositely circularly polarized THz radiation. Thus, we were able to unambiguously identify a temperature-dependent magnetic-like behavior of the KTaO₃ crystal manifested by the extracted Faraday rotation. We developed a theoretical model describing well quantitatively the measured curves of the transient Faraday effect signal. However, their amplitudes exhibit an unexpected temperature dependence, which might be a key to a deeper understanding of the observed phonomagnetic effect.

    💡 亮点：圆偏振太赫兹脉冲以约 300 kV/cm 电场共振驱动 KTaO3 的简并软极性声子，并在抗磁材料中产生瞬态类磁响应。关键机制是极性声子角动量或手性运动向磁响应的转移，而非静态磁序。该工作直接连接团队的声子、自旋晶格耦合和超快动力学研究，但摘要未给出响应寿命和微观耦合常数。

    通过抑制交替磁性产生自旋三重态配对#
    Spin triplet pairing by suppressing altermagnetism#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Xin Ma, Siqi Wu, Zilong Li, Lunhui Hu, Jianhui Dai, Chao Cao🔥 AI 95
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：非常规超导性与交错磁序之间的相互作用已经引起广泛关注。尤其是，通过抑制交错磁性是否能够实现自旋三重态超导，仍是一个悬而未决的问题。我们利用具有空位超结构的方格子最小单轨道 Hubbard 模型研究了这一问题；在该模型中，常规反铁磁序和交错磁序可以在平等的基础上出现。我们说明，由于几何阻挫和库仑相互作用，即使在半填充条件下，也可以存在具有交错磁性的金属正常相。利用电荷掺杂抑制交错磁长程有序，可以产生常规反铁磁自旋涨落和交错磁自旋涨落。当常规反铁磁自旋涨落占主导时，总是偏好自旋单重态配对。然而，当交错磁自旋涨落占主导时，则会诱导自旋三重态配对。我们还简要讨论了这些结果对于可能材料候选体系的意义。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2509.09959v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: The interplay between unconventional superconductivity and altermagnetic order has attracted much attention. In particular, whether spin-triplet superconductivity can be achieved by suppressing altermagnetism remains an open issue. We investigate this issue using a minimal single-orbital Hubbard model on a square lattice with a vacancy superstructure, in which both conventional antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic orders can emerge on an equal footing. We illustrate the existence of a metallic normal phase with altermagnetism even at half-filling due to geometric frustration and Coulomb interaction. Suppressing the altermagnetic long-range order using charge doping can lead to both conventional antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic spin fluctuations. Spin-singlet pairing is always favored when conventional antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations dominate. However, when altermagnetic spin fluctuations dominate, spin-triplet pairing will be induced. Implications of our results for possible material candidates are also briefly discussed.

    💡 亮点：单轨道 Hubbard 空位超结构同时容纳常规反铁磁和交替磁序，作者据此考察压制交替磁性后形成自旋三重态配对的条件。模型分析覆盖半填充附近的交替磁金属态与超导竞争，但输入没有给出配对对称性、临界温度或数值算法。它与团队的交替磁性和超导研究直接相关，主要价值在于提供可检验的相竞争模型。

    层状 NiPS3 的磁导率与区折叠声子#
    Magnetic permeability and zone-folded phonons in layered NiPS₃#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 André A. A. Silva, Eduardo D. Stefanato, Nicolas M. Kawahala, Flávio H. Feres, João Vítor T. P. Vital, Bernardo R. A. Neves, Jan Plutnar, Zdenek Sofer, Ana C. F. Brito, Felix G. G. Hernandez, Raul O. Freitas🔥 AI 99
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：三硫化镍磷（NiPS3）是一种具有XY型反铁磁有序的莫特绝缘体，表现出突出的物性。在本工作中，我们利用太赫兹时域光谱学研究了单晶NiPS3随温度变化的电动力学响应。当磁导率不能近似为1时，传统的太赫兹透射分析通常只能得到复折射率，从而阻碍了磁性贡献与介电贡献的区分。为克服这一限制，我们展示了一种能够将磁导率与电介电常数解耦的提取方法。在假设奈尔温度TN以下介电贡献保持不变的情况下，我们利用顺磁相的数据分离出13 K时的本征磁性成分。光谱的角度和温度依赖性揭示了一个接近1 THz的磁振子模（对于0°取向），以及一个由对称性破缺激活的区域折叠声子（在90°取向）。利用德鲁德-洛伦兹振子成功拟合了分离出的磁导率，从而能够提取该材料中的基本自旋动力学参数。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26405v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Nickel phosphorus trisulfide (NiPS₃₎ stands out as a Mott insulator exhibiting XY-type antiferromagnetic order. In this work, we investigate the electrodynamic response of single-crystalline NiPS₃ using Terahertz Time-Domain Spectroscopy (THz-TDS) as a function of temperature. In cases where the magnetic permeability cannot be approximated to unity, conventional THz transmission analysis is often restricted to the complex refractive index, hindering the distinction between magnetic and dielectric contributions. To overcome this limitation, we demonstrate an extraction methodology capable of decoupling the magnetic permeability from the electric permittivity. Assuming that the dielectric contribution remains invariant below the N'eel temperature (TN), we used data from the paramagnetic phase to isolate the intrinsic magnetic component at 13 K. The angular and thermal dependence of the spectra revealed a magnon mode near 1 THz (for 0ⁱ̧rc orientation) and, distinctively, a zone-folded phonon activated by symmetry breaking (at 90ⁱ̧rc). The isolated magnetic permeability was successfully modeled using Drude-Lorentz oscillators, enabling the extraction of fundamental spin dynamics parameters in this material.

    💡 亮点：NiPS3 的太赫兹响应同时包含磁导率、介电函数和区折叠声子，作者针对传统透射分析的混淆问题进行分离。对象是具有 XY 型反铁磁序的层状 Mott 绝缘体，测量手段为温度依赖太赫兹时域光谱。它与团队磁性、声子、自旋晶格耦合和低维材料方向相关，但摘要未提供拟合参数或异常温度。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

  14. 37
    通过自旋轨道力矩与本征各向异性实现非易失多态磁切换#
    Non-volatile Multistate Magnetic Switching via Spin-orbit Torque and Intrinsic Anisotropy#
    📊 含图深析
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Fei Ye, Chunzheng Wang, Xue Zhang, Sihai Jiao, Zhongjie Wang, Long Cheng, Zhifeng Zhu, Chunlei Gao, Xiaofang Zhai
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：尽管电流诱导的双态自旋轨道力矩（SOT）切换已经得到充分确立，但对多个磁态进行确定性电控仍是自旋电子学中的核心挑战。在此，我们在 SrIrO₃/SrRuO₃ 双层膜中实现了一种概念全新的多态 SOT 器件；该器件包含四种本征稳定且可通过电学区分的磁态，包括两种面内倾斜态（IP_c^±）和两种面外倾斜态（OP_c^±）。脉冲电流激发完整映射了这四种状态之间全部 12 种确定性转变，从而建立了由两个特征电流密度定义的稳健切换协议。原位扫描氮空位（NV）中心磁力计提供了此前未观测到的 IP_c^± 状态的直接实空间证据；自旋动力学模拟则揭示了一个两步切换路径，该路径由自旋力矩与四重各向异性能景观中的有效各向异性场协同驱动。我们对本征多态 SOT 器件的展示直接回应了传统双态 SOT 技术的密度瓶颈，并为紧凑、高速和高能效的多态自旋电子学确立了一种有力范式。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2603.11656v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: While current-induced bistate spin-orbit torque (SOT) switching has been well established, deterministic electrical control of multiple magnetic states remains a central challenge in spintronics. Here, we realize a conceptually new multistate SOT device in a SrIrO₃/SrRuO₃ bilayer, hosting four intrinsically stable yet electrically distinguishable magnetic states, including two in-plane canted (IP_c^±) and two out-of-plane canted (OP_c^±) states. Pulsed current excitations fully map all twelve deterministic transitions among the four states, establishing a robust switching protocol defined by two characteristic current densities. In-situ scanning nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry provides direct real-space evidence for the previously unobserved IP_c^± states, and spin dynamics simulations uncover a two-step switching pathway, driven by the concerted action of spin torques and the effective anisotropy field within the fourfold anisotropy landscape. Our demonstration of the intrinsic multistate SOT device directly addresses the density bottleneck of conventional bistate SOT technology, establishing a powerful paradigm for compact, high-speed, and energy-efficient multistate spintronics.

    💡 亮点：SrIrO3/SrRuO3 双层实现四种非易失磁态：两种面内和两种面外倾斜态，并通过脉冲电流完成四态间十二种确定性转变。工作把自旋轨道力矩从二态开关扩展到由本征各向异性保护的多态操控。它与团队自旋电子学、磁各向异性和多场调控直接相关，但摘要未给出电流阈值、保持时间或微观力矩分解。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”补充训练/验证集，再比较《通过自旋轨道力矩与本征各向异性实现非易失多态磁切换》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题如何把传统自旋轨道力矩的二态磁翻转，推进为可稳定存在、可电学寻址、可确定性切换的多态磁写入，从而突破二值存储的信息密度瓶颈。
    创新方法利用 SrIrO3/SrRuO3 双层中的本征四重各向异性能量景观，把自旋轨道力矩与有效各向异性场协同起来，构建出四个本征稳定且电学可区分的磁态，并实现 12 条确定性态间跃迁。
    工作流程施加脉冲电流驱动磁化翻转，扫描不同电流区间建立四态切换图谱，再用原位 NV 中心磁显微直接观测局域磁态，最后结合自旋动力学模拟重建两步式切换路径。
    关键结果识别出四个稳定磁态：IP_c⁺、IP_c⁻、OP_c⁺、OP_c⁻；完整映射全部 12 条确定性跃迁；提炼出两个特征电流密度作为写入窗口；原位 NV 测量直接证实此前未观察到的面内倾斜态。
    应用价值为高密度、非易失、多比特磁存储提供了可工程化的物理基础，把 SOT 器件从二态开关推进到本征多态写入，适合面向高集成度自旋电子器件与多级存储单元。

    以下基于你提供的题目、摘要与 arXiv 元数据；未提供全文，因此无法逐章核对原始章节标题，也不能可靠给出全文中的逐段引述、样本量、P 值或模型超参数。下面先做高密度解析。

    第一部分：核心概览

    这篇工作把自旋轨道力矩（SOT）从传统的二态翻转推进到四态非易失多状态写入：在 SrIrO3/SrRuO3 双层中，实现了四个本征稳定且电学可区分的磁态，包括两个面内倾斜态与两个面外倾斜态。其关键贡献不只是“多了几个态”，而是给出了一套可重复映射 12 条确定性态间跃迁 的电流协议，并用 in-situ NV 中心磁测量 直接看见此前未观察到的面内倾斜态，再用自旋动力学证明切换来自 SOT 与四重各向异性能量景观中的有效各向异性场 的协同作用。领域地位上，这属于把 SOT 器件从单比特式二值存储，推进到更高信息密度、多态可编程磁器件的代表性路线。

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《Non-volatile Multistate Magnetic Switching via Spin-orbit Torque and Intrinsic Anisotropy》（None，）

    背景：传统电流驱动 SOT 切换已成熟，但稳定、确定性、可寻址的多磁态控制仍是瓶颈。多态磁器件的价值在于直接提升信息密度。

    方法：在 SrIrO3/SrRuO3 双层中构建多态磁系统；施加脉冲电流扫描切换；用原位扫描 NV 中心磁显微验证局域磁态；以自旋动力学模拟重建切换路径。

    结果：识别出四个稳定磁态：IP_c^± 与 OP_c^±。全部 12 个确定性跃迁 被电流脉冲完整映射；提炼出两个特征电流密度作为切换协议的核心参数；NV 测量直接证实 IP_c^± 的存在。

    讨论：切换不是简单的单步翻转，而是由两步式路径主导，体现 SOT、有效各向异性场 与 四重各向异性 的共同决定作用。

    局限：当前可见信息只显示了原型层面的物理演示；未给出全文中的能耗、速度、循环寿命、器件良率、热稳定性窗口等关键工程指标。

    结论：实现了一个内禀多态 SOT 器件，直接针对传统二态 SOT 的容量瓶颈，给出面向高密度、快速、低能耗多态自旋电子学的新范式。

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析

    由于未提供全文，无法严格按原始章节标题逐章展开；下面按论文最可能的逻辑结构，对摘要中可见内容做“章节级”冷凝。

    研究问题与领域瓶颈

    核心问题是：如何把电流控制磁态从稳定的二态写入推进到可确定寻址的多态写入。摘要明确指出 *"deterministic electrical control of multiple magnetic states remains a central challenge in spintronics"*。这里的关键不是“能不能翻转”，而是能否在同一器件中实现多个磁态的稳定共存、可区分读出、确定性切换。传统 bistate SOT 虽然已成熟，但每个器件只能承载有限信息，直接受限于存储密度。

    器件平台与材料选择

    器件基于 SrIrO3/SrRuO3 bilayer。摘要给出的定位是 *"hosting four intrinsically stable yet electrically distinguishable magnetic states"*。这里的“intrinsically stable”很重要，意味着这些态不是依赖外加场临时维持，而是由材料本征磁各向异性与磁各向间耦合共同支撑。四个态分成两类：两个in-plane canted (IP_c^±)，两个out-of-plane canted (OP_c^±)。这说明系统不是简单的平面内/平面外二分，而是具有倾斜磁化分量的多能谷结构。

    多态定义与态空间

    摘要直接给出四态命名：IP_c⁺、IP_c⁻、OP_c⁺、OP_c⁻。每个“±”代表一个对称破缺方向，说明器件内部至少存在一个可用于电学寻址的极性自由度。四态结构比二态系统多出一倍以上的信息承载维度；若按最小编码思想，四态可对应两比特信息单元，但前提是读写确定性足够高。

    切换协议与电流阈值

    摘要写明 *"Pulsed current excitations fully map all twelve deterministic transitions among the four states"*，并且 *"defined by two characteristic current densities"*。这说明切换行为可以由两个关键电流密度区域分段描述，而不是每条跃迁都需要独立标定。这里的技术意义很强：多态器件若没有低维协议，会变成不可用的复杂相图；而两个特征电流密度意味着器件存在可操作的分层写入规则，可被工程化为编程窗口。

    实验直接证据

    摘要指出 *"In-situ scanning nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry provides direct real-space evidence for the previously unobserved IP_c^± states"*。这类证据的级别高于仅靠输运信号反演，因为 NV 磁测量提供了实空间磁场分布。关键点是“previously unobserved”：IP_c^± 不是仅靠模型拟合猜出来，而是被直接看见。对于多态磁器件，这种可视化证据能显著提高态定义的可信度。

    机理解释与理论模拟

    摘要给出 *"spin dynamics simulations uncover a two-step switching pathway"*，并指出驱动力是 *"the concerted action of spin torques and the effective anisotropy field within the fourfold anisotropy landscape"*。这表明切换机制不是单次力矩脉冲直接把磁化矢量推到目标态，而是先跨越某个中间能谷，再由各向异性场收束到最终稳定态。two-step pathway 是理解该器件的核心术语，它解释了为什么多个态能被确定性地访问，而不是随机落入邻近极小值。

    学术结论与应用指向

    摘要最后将系统定义为 *"intrinsic multistate SOT device"*，并把意义概括为 *"directly addresses the density bottleneck of conventional bistate SOT technology"*。这句话的含义很直接：新的贡献不只是演示一种奇异磁态，而是在器件层面打开了更高存储密度、更小占地、更快写入的可能性。它的定位接近“从物理可行性到器件范式”的过渡。

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持

    1. 术语解析

    • Spin-orbit torque (SOT)：由电流在强自旋轨道耦合材料中诱发的力矩，用来驱动磁化翻转。这里它不是背景技术，而是多态寻址的执行器。
    • Intrinsic anisotropy：本征各向异性，强调态稳定性来自材料自身能量景观，不依赖外场临时锁定。
    • Canted state：倾斜态，磁化方向既不完全平面内，也不完全垂直，而是处于折中角度。这种状态常对应多个能谷之间的竞争结果。
    • Deterministic transitions：确定性跃迁，意思是给定起始态和电流条件后，终态是可重复、可预测的，不是概率性随机翻转。
    • Fourfold anisotropy landscape：四重各向异性能量景观，指系统存在四个等价或近等价极小值，天然适合多态存储，但也更难实现可控写入。

    2. 疑难查证

    最容易混淆的是“four states”和“twelve transitions”。四态只是静态可达的磁配置数；十二个跃迁指的是状态间有向转换的数量。若把四态标成 A/B/C/D，理论上共有 4×3=12 条有向边，这正是摘要说的“fully map all twelve deterministic transitions”的直接含义。另一个容易误解的点是“two characteristic current densities”：它不等于只有两个开关电流阈值那么简单，更像是把复杂转变分成两个主导电流区间，每个区间对应不同的能量跨越方式与终态选择规则。

    第五部分：创新评估与关联

    新颖性判断：过去 2-5 年的相关 SOT 工作，大多集中在三条线：一是提高二态翻转效率；二是用外场、交换偏置或结构工程实现准多态；三是探索反铁磁、铁磁/亚铁磁复合体系中的更复杂切换。相比之下，这篇工作的新意在于把多态性做成材料本征属性，并且用原位 NV 磁成像给出面内倾斜态的直接证据，再用两步切换机制解释完整的 12 条跃迁图谱。真正解决的是两个前人长期没同时解决的问题：多态的稳定性与多态的确定性寻址。很多系统能“看到多个态”，但难以稳定重现；也有系统能“切换”，但态的物理可验证性不足。这里把两者合在了一起。

    需要补充的全文信息

    CoHo 亚铁磁合金中 Co 3d 与 Ho 4f 自旋的激光超快动力学#
    Laser induced ultrafast Co 3d and Ho 4f spin dynamics in CoHo ferrimagnetic alloys#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Deeksha Gupta, Boonthum Kunyangyuen, Matthias Riepp, Karsten Holldack, Radu-Marius Abrudan, Torsten Kachel, Niko Pontius, Christian Schüßler-Langeheine, Mircea Vomir, Michel Hehn, Nicolas Bergeard🔥 AI 97
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：过渡金属（TM）/稀土（RE）合金近年来重新受到关注，因为它们是阐明飞秒激光脉冲诱导的全光螺旋度无关切换（AO-HIS）起源的模型体系。近期研究在CoDy和CoHo合金以及Co/Gd超薄双层中报道了一种独特的单脉冲磁化反转机制，该机制基于畴壁运动和合并。已有观点认为，当Co亚晶格完全退磁、稀土亚晶格的特征退磁时间τRE（Gd、Dy或Ho）长于Co亚晶格的退磁时间，并且传递给Co的角动量减少时，便可以实现这种特定的翻转切换。对CoDy合金开展的元素分辨和时间分辨X射线光谱研究表明，在室温下其特征退磁时间为τCo=0.2 ps和τDy=0.6 ps。类似地，预计Ho会表现出更慢的响应，但其超快4f自旋动力学此前尚未得到实验研究。在此，我们对亚铁磁Co80Ho20合金中飞秒激光诱导的Co 3d和Ho 4f自旋超快动力学进行了元素分辨和时间分辨研究，以验证这一预测。我们在室温下观测到特征退磁时间τCo=0.22±0.01 ps和τHo=0.87±0.15 ps。这些结果表明，在Co80Ho20中Ho的退磁明显慢于Co，从而支持了由畴壁介导的全光翻转切换所提出的条件。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27083v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The transition metal (TM) / rare-earth (RE) alloys have received renewed interest lately as model systems to decipher the origin of all-optical helicity-independent switching (AO-HIS) by femtosecond laser pulses. Recently, a distinct single-pulse magnetization reversal mechanism, based on domain-wall motion and coalescence, has been reported in CoDy and CoHo alloys, as well as in Co/Gd ultrathin bilayers. It has been claimed that this specific toggle switching is achieved when the Co sublattice is fully demagnetized and the characteristic demagnetization times τRE of the RE (Gd, Dy or Ho) sublattices is longer than that of the Co and the angular momentum transferred to Co is reduced. Element- and time-resolved X-ray spectroscopy studies of CoDy alloys have reported characteristic demagnetization times of τCo = 0.2 ps and τDy = 0.6 ps at room temperature. Similarly, Ho is expected to exhibit a slower response, but its ultrafast 4f spin dynamics remain experimentally unexplored. Here, we report on element- and time-resolved investigations of femtosecond laser induced ultrafast dynamics of the Co 3d and Ho 4f spins in ferrimagnetic Co₈₀Ho₂₀ alloys to verify this prediction. We observed characteristic demagnetization times τCo = 0.22 +/- 0.01 ps and τHo = 0.87 +/- 0.15 ps at room temperature. These results show that Ho demagnetizes substantially more slowly than Co in Co₈₀Ho₂₀, supporting the proposed condition for domain-wall-mediated all-optical toggle switching

    💡 亮点：CoHo 亚铁磁合金中 Co 3d 与 Ho 4f 自旋对飞秒激光的响应被分辨出来，用于检验与光 helicity 无关的全光翻转机制。摘要强调 Co 子晶格完全退磁以及畴壁运动、并合在翻转中的作用。该问题与团队非绝热自旋动力学、电子晶格耦合和磁性材料方向相关，但当前文本缺少实验时间尺度与定量磁矩。

    不同晶格几何三维及更高维 Hubbard 模型中的磁不稳定性#
    Magnetic instabilities in three- and higher-dimensional Hubbard models with different lattice geometries: Impact of nonlocal spatial correlations#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Marvin Leusch, Alessandro Toschi, Andreas Hausoel, Giorgio Sangiovanni, Georg Rohringer🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们分析了三维、四维和五维中各种布拉维晶格的晶格几何对强相互作用电子体系磁有序相热力学转变的影响，同时考虑局域和非局域关联效应。首先，我们使用只纳入局域关联的动力学平均场理论（DMFT），计算 Hubbard 模型在三维简单立方晶格（3d-sc）、四维简单立方或超立方晶格（4d-sc）、五维简单立方或超立方晶格（5d-sc），以及三维体心立方晶格（bcc）和面心立方晶格（fcc）上的磁化率，并确定相应磁有序态的转变温度。第二步，我们采用动力学顶角近似（DΓA）这一 DMFT 的图示扩展，以纳入非局域关联的影响；在相应相变附近，非局域关联尤为重要。对于二分的 3d-sc、4d-sc、5d-sc 和 bcc 晶格，非局域涨落会显著降低 DMFT 转变温度，这与平均场方法通常高估有序相稳定性的总体趋势一致。正如预期，随着配位数增加，DMFT 与 DΓA 转变温度之间的差异幅度逐渐减小，因为 DMFT 在大配位数或高维极限下是精确的。从更实际的角度看，这些结果也为评估不同材料几何中 DMFT 有序温度预期高估程度提供了合理参考。然而，对于 fcc 晶格，DMFT 中观察到的有序相在 DΓA 中完全消失，这与该晶格中存在强几何阻挫相一致。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2512.14396v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We analyze the impact of the lattice geometry on the thermodynamic transition to magnetically ordered phases in strongly interacting electron systems for various Bravais lattices in three, four and five dimensions, including both local and nonlocal correlation effects. In a first step we use the dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), which takes into account purely local correlations, to calculate the magnetic susceptibilities of the Hubbard model on three- (3d-sc), four- (4d-sc), and five-dimensional (5d-sc) simple cubic/hypercubic, as well as on three-dimensional body- (bcc) and face-centered (fcc) cubic lattices, and determine the transition temperature to the corresponding magnetically ordered state. In a second step, we exploit the dynamical vertex approximation (DΓA), a diagrammatic extension of DMFT, to include the effect of nonlocal correlations which are particularly important in the vicinity of the corresponding phase transition. For the bipartite 3d-sc, 4d-sc, 5d-sc and bcc lattices nonlocal fluctuations lead to a substantial reduction of the DMFT transition temperature consistent to the overall tendency of mean-field approaches to overestimate the stability of ordered phases. As expected, the magnitude of the difference between the DMFT, being exact in the limit of large connectivity/dimensions, and DΓA transition temperatures decreases with increasing coordination number. On a more practical perspective, these results also provide a reasonable guidance to evaluate the expected overestimation of the DMFT ordering temperature for different material geometries. For the fcc lattice, on the other hand, the ordered phase observed in DMFT vanishes completely within DΓA which is consistent with the existence of strong geometric frustration in this lattice.

    💡 亮点：文章比较不同晶格几何和空间维度下 Hubbard 模型的磁易感性与磁有序转变，并区分局域与非局域空间关联。方法以 DMFT 为起点，覆盖三维简单立方及四、五维超立方晶格。它与团队材料磁性模型的直接联系有限，主要可用于理解有效自旋模型和蒙特卡洛结果中的维度、几何与关联效应，摘要没有具体材料结论。

    螺旋对称分子中的自旋保持#
    Spin preservation in screw-symmetric molecules#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jonas Bloch, Fedor Baranov, Maxim Breitkreiz🔥 AI 94
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：在长分子中的电子输运过程中，通常认为只有当驻留时间远短于特征自旋混合时间尺度ℏ/Δ时，自旋才能得到保持，其中Δ是某种自旋依赖势的大小，例如自旋轨道耦合的强度。我们证明，在具有离散螺旋对称性的分子中，由于准动量空间中存在强烈的自旋分离，自旋保持能力可以显著增强，远远超过这一时间尺度。长分子中的这种自旋保真度与手性诱导自旋选择性相一致，表明在长手性分子中可能存在由增强的自旋分裂机制驱动的自旋依赖输运。我们给出了增强自旋保持的解析推导，并在紧束缚模型中对其进行了检验；结果证实，当螺旋对称性被破坏，或从离散螺旋对称性变为连续螺旋对称性时，该效应会逐渐减弱。此外，我们进行输运模拟，结果表明，在具有两个磁性电极的自旋阀构型中，会出现由对称性保护的高保真自旋输运所产生的显著磁阻曲线；我们提出，这可以作为一种实验上可实现的特征信号。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27002v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In electronic transport through long molecules, spin is expected to be preserved only when the dwell time is much shorter than the characteristic spin-mixing timescale hbar/Δ, where Δ is the magnitude of a spin-dependent potential, such as spin-orbit coupling. We show that, in molecules featuring discrete screw symmetry, spin preservation can be enhanced far beyond this timescale owing to strong spin separation in quasi-momentum. This spin fidelity in long molecules is consistent with chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS), suggesting spin-dependent transport in long, chiral molecules with amplified spin-splitting mechanisms. We provide analytical derivation of the enhanced spin preservation and test it on tight-binding models, which confirm that the effect gradually weakens when the screw symmetry is broken or changes from discrete to continuous. Furthermore, we perform transport simulations to show that a strong magnetoresistance trace of symmetry-protected spin fidelity emerges in a spin-valve setup with two magnetic leads, which we propose as an experimentally accessible signature.

    💡 亮点：离散螺旋对称分子可通过准动量空间中的强自旋分离，延长自旋保持时间并超越 hbar/Δ 估计。作者把这一机制与手性诱导自旋选择性联系起来，重点是对称性保护的输运通道。它与团队材料自旋输运和对称性建模只有间接关系，不涉及团队常用的晶体缺陷、磁性材料或机器学习电子结构。

    非对称耦合量子点—拓扑超导纳米线结中的 Majorana 信号#
    Majorana signatures in an asymmetrically coupled quantum dot--topological superconducting nanowire junction#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Levente Máthé, Ioan Grosu, Liviu P. Zârbo, Ionel Ţifrea🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    4

    📄 摘要：我们对一个纳米尺度体系中的量子输运进行了理论研究。在该体系中，中心量子点（QD）与正常电极不对称耦合，同时与穿过可调磁通的拓扑超导纳米线两端局域的两个马约拉纳束缚态（MBS）耦合。我们考察了电极与量子点之间的耦合不对称参数 α，以及偏压不对称参数 q 对体系线性电导的影响，并分别研究了 MBS 未杂化和已杂化的情形。在零温极限下，对于未杂化的 MBS，只有当磁通相位 φ=(2n+1)π（n∈Z）时体系的线性电导才是有限的，其表达式为 G=2qαe²/[h(α+1)]；对于已杂化的 MBS，线性电导则呈现出对体系参数的复杂依赖。在有限温度下，对于未杂化的 MBS，体系线性电导随磁通相位 φ 以 2π 为周期振荡；仅仅改变偏压不对称参数 q 的数值，就可以使线性电导极大值的位置从 φ=2nπ 移动到 φ=(2n+1)π。对于已杂化的 MBS，当中心量子点的能级 ε_d 被调节到电极费米能级处（ε_d=ε_F）时，电导表现出类似行为；然而，当 ε_d≠ε_F 时，振荡周期会变为 4π，并且线性电导极大值的位置取决于 ε_d 的具体数值以及体系中的其他参数。我们的结果强调了电极-量子点耦合不对称参数和偏压不对称参数在实验中的重要性；这些参数通常存在于真实实验装置中，并且会强烈影响对 MBS 输运特征的识别和观测。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26341v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We present a theoretical study of the quantum transport through a nanoscale system in which a central quantum dot (QD) is coupled asymmetrically to normal leads and to two Majorana bound states (MBSs) localized at the ends of a topological superconducting nanowire threaded by a tunable magnetic flux. The effects of the leads--QD coupling asymmetry parameter α and the bias voltage asymmetry parameter q on the system's linear conductance are considered for the case of unhybridized and hybridized MBSs. In the zero-temperature limit, for unhybridized MBSs the system's linear conductance is finite only when the magnetic flux phase φ = (2n+1)π (ninZ) and it scales as G=2qα e²/[h(α+1)], while for hybridized MBSs it presents a complicated dependence on the system's parameters. At finite temperature, for unhybridized MBSs, the system's linear conductance oscillates as a function of the magnetic flux phase φ with a period of 2π, and the position of the linear conductance maxima can be shifted from φ=2nπ to φ=(2n+1)π by simply varying the value of the bias voltage asymmetry parameter q. For hybridized MBSs, the conductance exhibits a similar behavior when the energy level of the central QD, ǎrepsilon_d, is tuned at the leads' Fermi level (ǎrepsilon_d=ǎrepsilon_F), although when ǎrepsilon_d≠ǎrepsilon_F the oscillation period changes to 4π, and the position of the linear conductance maxima depends on the actual value of ǎrepsilon_d and other parameters in the system. Our results highlight the experimental importance of the leads-QD and bias voltage asymmetry parameters, which are often present in realistic experimental setups, and can strongly affect the identification and observation of MBSs transport signatures.

    💡 亮点：模型考察量子点、非对称正常电极和拓扑超导纳米线两端 Majorana 束缚态之间的耦合，并通过磁通调节干涉输运。核心变量是耦合不对称 α、偏压不对称 q 以及 Majorana 是否杂化，观测量为零温线性电导。与团队超导、BdG 和量子输运方法存在方法交叉，但与团队主攻材料体系没有直接对应。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    室温各向同性纳米尺度量子传感器#
    Isotropic Nanoscale Quantum Sensor at Room-Temperature#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Daniel T. Möller, Baha Sakar, Ekrem T. Güldeste, Dhruv Wadhwani, Olt Gashi, Daniel A. García Vaca, Mokessh K. Ciwan, Simone P. Crozier, Christian Laube, Sirswa K. Shree Ram, Matteo Slaviero, Nabeel Aslam🔥 AI 84
    相关度
    4

    📄 摘要：基于色心的量子传感器能够在环境条件下提供纳米尺度分辨率，但其适用性仍受到限制。由于量子化轴被锁定在宿主晶格上，传统色心在偏离轴向的磁场中会遭受严重的信号损失。为解决这一问题，我们报告了一种由金刚石中电中性氮空位中心（NV0）实现的各向同性磁力计。在这里，自旋—电荷动力学产生了一个 NV0 暗自旋对，其量子化轴会动态地与外部磁场对齐。通过 NV 电荷态选择性荧光进行读出，该系统在室温下表现出微秒级相干性，并且对于任意磁场方向都具有纳特斯拉灵敏度。我们展示了无需对准即可绘制陡峭磁场梯度和单个顺磁微目标的磁场图，同时还实现了对随机取向纳米金刚石的各向同性读出。该平台解决了长期存在的取向限制，为生命科学和量子材料中的不受方向约束的纳米尺度磁力测量开辟了道路。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26942v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Color-center based quantum sensors provide nanoscale resolution under ambient conditions, yet their applicability remains limited. Because the quantization axes are locked to the host lattice, conventional color centers suffer severe signal loss in off-axis magnetic fields. To address this, we report an isotropic magnetometer enabled by the neutrally charged nitrogen-vacancy center (NV0) in diamond. Here, spin-to-charge dynamics yield an NV0-dark spin pair whose quantization axis dynamically aligns with the external field. Read out through NV charge-state-selective fluorescence, this system exhibits microsecond room-temperature coherence and nanotesla sensitivity for arbitrary field directions. We demonstrate alignment-free mapping of steep field gradients and single paramagnetic micro-targets, alongside isotropic readout from randomly oriented nanodiamonds. Resolving longstanding orientation constraints, this platform unlocks unrestricted nanoscale magnetometry across life sciences and quantum materials.

    无序三角晶格 Yb³⁺ 化合物 LiCaYb₅(BO₃)₆ 的低温磁性与自旋动力学#
    Low-temperature magnetism and spin dynamics in the disordered triangular-lattice Yb³⁺ compound LiCaYb₅₍BO₃₎₆#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Monika Jawale, Saikat Nandi, Prashanta K. Mukharjee, Philipp Gegenwart, A. V. Mahajan🔥 AI 96
    相关度
    3

    📄 摘要：几何阻挫、自旋-轨道耦合和结构无序之间的相互作用，可能在稀土三角晶格磁体中产生非常规磁基态。我们报告了无序三角晶格化合物 LiCaYb₅(BO₃)₆（LCYBO）的低温磁性和 ⁷Li 核磁共振研究。Rietveld 精修确认其具有六方 P6₅22 结构，并存在部分 Ca/Yb 反位无序以及分数 Li 占位。磁化率和磁化测量表明，该体系具有孤立良好的有效 Jₑff = 1/2 克拉默斯双重态和弱反铁磁相互作用（θ_CW 约为 -0.54 K）。比热在约 0.43 K 处显示出一个微弱异常，这表明短程关联磁性开始发展，而不是形成常规的长程有序。⁷Li 核磁共振谱在降温过程中显著展宽，与逐渐增强的非均匀内部磁场相一致。自旋-晶格弛豫表现出两个弛豫分量，这与由反位无序和竞争性交换路径产生的局域磁环境分布相一致。我们的结果确立了 LCYBO 是一种具有结构无序和受挫特征、并表现出关联低能自旋动力学的三角晶格磁体。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26946v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The interplay between geometric frustration, spin--orbit coupling, and structural disorder can give rise to unconventional magnetic ground states in rare-earth triangular-lattice magnets. We report low-temperature magnetic and ⁷Li NMR investigations of the disordered triangular-lattice compound LiCaYb₅₍BO₃₎₆ (LCYBO). Rietveld refinement confirms a hexagonal P6₅₂₂ structure with partial Ca/Yb antisite disorder and fractional Li occupancy. Magnetic susceptibility and magnetization measurements indicate a well-isolated effective Jₑff=1/2 Kramers doublet with weak antiferromagnetic interactions (θCW approx -0.54~K). The specific heat reveals a weak anomaly near 0.43 K, suggesting the development of short-range correlated magnetism rather than conventional long-range order. ⁷Li NMR spectra broaden strongly upon cooling, consistent with increasingly inhomogeneous internal magnetic fields. The spin-lattice relaxation exhibits two relaxation components consistent with disorder-induced distributions of local magnetic environments arising from antisite disorder and competing exchange pathways. Our results establish LCYBO as a structurally disordered frustrated triangular-lattice magnet with correlated low-energy spin dynamics.

    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 Preprints👤 Ben Stedman, Giampaolo D’Alessandro, Przemyslaw Kula, Malgosia Kaczmarek

    📄 摘要：铁电向列液晶（FNLC）将长程取向有序性与宏观极化结合在一起。尽管其铁电向列相已受到广泛关注，但其极性特征在多大程度上影响较高温度下的非极性向列相，仍基本未被探索。在此，我们通过介电和光学表征，研究了室温铁电向列混合物 MIX NF-P1 的向列相，重点考察其介电系数、双折射、电压阈值以及展曲和弯曲弹性常数随温度的变化。结果表明，尽管不存在铁电有序，该向列相中的电压阈值仍可比标准非极性向列液晶 E7 低至 20 倍，同时保持较大的介电各向异性。双折射随温度升高而单调降低，而弹性常数呈现出与传统向列液晶显著不同的温度依赖行为，其中展曲弹性常数相对保持不变。因此，我们证明铁电向列液晶的向列相不能被视为传统的向列状态，而是保留了潜在极性相互作用的特征。强介电响应与低阈值电压的结合，凸显了这类材料在高速电光和光子学应用中的潜力。

    📖 英文原文

    Ferroelectric nematic liquid crystals (FNLCs) combine long-range orientational order with macroscopic polarization. While their ferroelectric nematic phase has attracted considerable attention, the extent to which its polar character influences the higher-temperature non-polar nematic phase remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate the nematic phase of the room-temperature ferroelectric nematic mixture MIX NF-P1 through dielectric and optical characterization, in particular its dielectric coefficients, birefringence, voltage threshold, and the splay and bend elastic constants as a function of temperature. Voltage threshold is shown to be up to 20 times lower than in the standard non-polar nematic liquid crystal E7, along with the large dielectric anisotropy being retained in the non-polar nematic phase, in spite of the absence of ferroelectric order. Birefringence monotonically decreases with increasing temperature, while the elastic constants show temperature dependent behaviors that differ markedly from those observed in conventional nematics, with the splay elastic constant remaining relatively constant. Thus, we demonstrate that the nematic phase of FNLCs cannot be regarded as a conventional nematic state and instead retains signatures of the underlying polar interactions. The combination of strong dielectric response and a low threshold voltage highlights the potential of these materials for high-speed electro-optic and photonic applications.

    A continuous confinement-deconfinement transition in a triangular quantum magnet#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Suguru Hosoi, Sejun Park, Michihiro Hirata, Minseong Lee, Adam P Dioguardi, Joe D Thompson, Filip Ronning, Allen O Scheie, Kumpei Imamura, Kenichiro Hashimoto, Takasada Shibauchi, Bishnu P Belbase, Arjun Unnikrishnan, Johannes Knolle, Arnab Banerjee, Yuji Matsuda🔥 AI 88

    📄 摘要：承载不同激发的相之间的连续相变——玻色型磁振子与费米型自旋子之间的相变——是量子磁性领域长期寻求的现象，其类似于量子色动力学中的禁闭—解禁闭相变。我们报告了在三角晶格反铁磁体 TlYbS₂ 中观察到这类相变的证据。反铁磁序在低于 T_N 约 0.53 K 时形成。沿 c 轴的磁场抑制了这种有序，并在 μ₀H_c 约 3 T 以上将体系驱动进入具有自旋子费米面的无能隙量子自旋液体态；有限的热导率残余线性项、类泡利磁化率以及与温度无关的核磁共振奈特位移为此提供了证据。从高于 H_c 的一侧接近 H_c 时，巡游激发的散射率显著增强，而其态密度没有出现临界增强，这与传统磁性量子临界现象不同。这些结果指向一种由分数化激发支配、超越金兹堡—朗道范式的连续禁闭—解禁闭相变。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27411v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: A continuous transition between phases hosting distinct excitations---bosonic magnons versus fermionic spinons---is a long-sought phenomenon in quantum magnetism, analogous to the confinement--deconfinement transition in quantum chromodynamics. We report evidence for such a transition in the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet TlYbS₂. Antiferromagnetic order develops below T_N approx 0.53,K. A c-axis field suppresses this order, driving the system into a gapless quantum spin liquid with a spinon Fermi surface above μ₀ H_c approx 3,T, evidenced by a finite residual linear term in thermal conductivity, a Pauli-like susceptibility, and a temperature-independent NMR Knight shift. Approaching H_c from above, the scattering rate of itinerant excitations is strongly enhanced while their density of states shows no critical enhancement, atypical of conventional magnetic quantum criticality. These results point to a continuous confinement--deconfinement transition governed by fractionalized excitations beyond the Ginzburg--Landau paradigm.

    Fe-Se-H化合物的高温超导性#
    High-Temperature Superconductivity of the Fe-Se-H compound#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 S. I. Bondarenko, A. A. Prokhorov, N. N. Galtsov, V. P. Timofeev, V. P. Koverya, I. S. Bondarenko, A. V. Krevsun🔥 AI 82

    📄 摘要：使用EPR谱仪，在3.6-25 K和295 K温度下，对一种粉末状Fe-Se-H化合物测量了其在最高6000 Oe磁场中微波功率吸收随磁场的依赖关系；该化合物通过将氢热扩散进入临界温度为8 K的FeSe单晶而获得。这些依赖关系的形状以及对测量结果的分析确认了Fe-Se-H化合物在常压下的超导性，既包括3.6-25 K温度范围，也包括295 K，即室温。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27349v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Using an EPR spectrometer, the dependences of microwave power absorption on magnetic field up to 6000 Oe were measured at temperatures of 3.6-25 K and 295 K in a powdered compound Fe-Se-H, obtained by thermal diffusion of hydrogen into a FeSe single crystal with a critical temperature of 8 K. The shape of the dependences and the analysis of the measurement results confirm the superconductivity of the Fe-Se-H compound at normal pressure, both at temperatures of 3.6-25 K and at 295 K, i.e., at room temperature.

    成金属量子临界性：有能隙相之间转变中的费米面成核#
    Metallogenic quantum criticality: Fermi surface nucleation at transitions between gapped phases#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zhengyan Darius Shi🔥 AI 84
    相关度
    1

    📄 摘要：相互作用多体系统中的连续量子相变表现出在很大程度上独立于微观细节的普适现象。从伊辛模型中的磁性相变等经典实例中提炼出的一个组织原则是：有能隙相之间转变的低能物理由具有尺度不变性的量子场论支配，其无能隙自由度位于动量空间中的孤立点。在本文中，我们表明这一原则可能以一种根本性的方式失效：在分隔两个完全有能隙绝缘体的量子临界点上，整个无能隙费米面都可以出现。这类奇异相变被称为成金属量子临界点（MGQCP）。我们为不同分数陈绝缘体之间的 MGQCP 构造了一族受控的有效场论，并提出一种具有周期调制的量子霍尔双层体系，作为最小的微观实现。该临界理论呈现出不同寻常的物理特征组合：输运和压缩率遵循与共形场论相容的标度定律，而电子谱函数在费米动量处发展出幂律非解析性，这是非费米液体金属的典型特征。我们讨论了 MGQCP 概念如何为具有可调陈能带的莫尔材料中的临界现象提供新的认识。更一般地说，我们的结果指向一类更广泛的量子临界现象：尽管相邻两个相中都不存在动量空间的延展无能隙激发流形，但在临界点却会出现这类流形。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27453v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed metallogenic quantum critical points (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

    💡 亮点：相互作用多体系统中的连续量子相变表现出在很大程度上独立于微观细节的普适现象。从伊辛模型中的磁性相变等经典实例中提炼出的一个组织原则是：有能隙相之间转变的低能物理由具有尺度不变性的量子场论支配，其无能隙自由度位于动量空间中的孤立点。在本文中，我们表明这一原则可能以一种根本性的方式失效：在分隔两个完全有能隙绝缘体的量子临界点上，整个无能隙费米面都可以出现。这类奇异相变被称为成金属量子临界点（MGQCP）。我们为不同分数陈绝缘体之间的 MGQCP 构造了一族受控的有效场论，并提出一…

    磁性 Weyl 半金属 Co₃Sn₂S₂ 中的太赫兹反常霍尔效应#
    Terahertz anomalous Hall effect in magnetic Weyl semimetal Co₃Sn₂S₂#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ashutosh Singh, Hongjing Xu, Xielin Wang, Kohei Fujiwara, Atsushi Tsukazaki, Shengxi Huang, Andrey Baydin, Junichiro Kono, Alexey Belyanin🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：时间反演对称性被破缺的 Weyl 半金属在其电子能带结构中至少具有两个节点，这些节点在动量空间中分离，并充当 Berry 曲率的源与汇。这会产生横向霍尔电导，即反常霍尔效应（AHE）；在最简单的双节点图像中，在零频极限下它与节点在动量空间中的分离成正比。在最近发现的 Weyl 半金属 Co₃Sn₂S₂ 中，已经观察到巨大的 AHE。然而，直接探测 Weyl 节点附近准粒子激发的低能区实验研究仍然有限。本文结合太赫兹光谱和基于物理直观有效模型的半解析计算，对 Co₃Sn₂S₂ 的本征低能旋光光学响应进行了系统研究。我们的结果为所观察到的磁光现象提供了稳健而清晰的解释，即这些现象源于 Weyl 节点在动量空间中的分离所导致的本征旋光性。此外，实验与理论之间的定量比较对材料参数施加了严格约束。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27195v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Time-reversal-symmetry-broken Weyl semimetals are known to have at least two nodes in their electronic band structure, separated in momentum space and acting as sources and sinks of Berry curvature. This gives rise to a transverse Hall conductivity, known as the anomalous Hall effect (AHE), which, in the simplest two-node picture, is proportional to the momentum-space separation between the nodes in the zero frequency limit. In the recently discovered Weyl semimetal Co₃Sn₂S₂, a giant AHE has been observed. However, experimental investigations in the low-energy regime, which directly probe quasiparticle excitations near the Weyl nodes, remain limited. Here, we present a systematic study of the intrinsic low-energy gyrotropic optical response of Co₃Sn₂S₂ using terahertz spectroscopy combined with semianalytical calculations based on a physically intuitive effective model. Our results provide a robust and transparent explanation of the observed magnetooptical phenomena in terms of intrinsic gyrotropy arising from momentum-space separation of the Weyl nodes. Furthermore, quantitative comparison between experiment and theory places stringent constraints on the material parameters.

    具有电子与晶格不稳定性的反铁磁金属 GdB6 的三维磁相图（H-θ-φ）#
    3D- (H-theta-phi) magnetic phase diagram of antiferromagnetic metal GdB6 with electron and lattice instability#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 A. N. Azarevich, A. V. Bogach, T. F. Garipova, V. V. Voronov, M. Rajnak, S. Gabani, K. Flachbart, N. B. Bolotina, O. N. Khrykina, V. M. Gridchina, A. Yu. Tsvetkov, S. Yu. Gavrilkin, N. E. Sluchanko🔥 AI 97
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：在具有立方晶格和 Gd S 型磁离子的反铁磁（AF）金属 GdB6 中，研究了电荷输运和磁化各向异性的起源（Néel 温度 TN ~ 15.5 K）。精确的低温 X 射线衍射测量发现了小的静态 Jahn-Teller 畸变和纳米尺度的电子不稳定性（动态电荷条纹）。通过详细的磁场（H）-温度（T）相图构建，识别出两个主要的磁相 AF(I) 和 AF(II)。利用磁阻和磁化的角度 φ 依赖性，在液氦温度下发现在（110）和（111）平面中的 H-φ 磁相图呈现出类似叶轮的图案，其中 AF 相 I 和 II 由径向和圆形边界彼此分隔。这些结果支持 AF(II) 态磁结构中自旋密度波 5d- 分量发挥重要作用的观点。作者提出，条纹中的电荷涨落可能导致沿 <100> 和 <110> 方向排列的第一、第二近邻 Gd3+ 离子之间的 Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yoshida（RKKY）间接交换受到抑制。这些动态电荷条纹以及受振动耦合的 Gd-Gd 对共同导致了异常的电荷散射各向异性和 GdB6 中具有 S 型磁离子的叶轮式图样。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27134v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The origin of charge transport and magnetization anisotropy was studied in GdB6, an antiferromagnetic (AF) metal (Néel temperature TN ~ 15.5 K) with cubic lattice and Gd S-type magnetic ions. Both small static Jahn-Teller distortions and nanoscale electronic instabilities (dynamic charge stripes) were found in precise low temperature X-ray diffraction measurements. The detailed magnetic field (H) vs temperature (T) phase diagrams were constructed with two main magnetic phases AF(I) and AF(II). Using the angular phi-dependences of magnetoresistance and magnetization, impeller-type patterns of the H-phi magnetic phase diagrams in the (110) and (111) planes were found at helium temperatures, which included the AF phases I and II separated from each other by radial and circular boundaries. The results argue in favor of the important role of the spin density wave 5d- component in the magnetic structure of AF(II) state. Charge fluctuations in stripes are proposed to be responsible for the suppression of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yoshida (RKKY) indirect exchange between the first and second neighboring Gd3+ ions located along the <100> and <110> directions. These dynamic charge stripes and vibrationally coupled Gd-Gd pairs produce unusual anisotropy of charge scattering and the impeller-type diagrams in GdB6 with S-type magnetic ion.

    分数量子霍尔效应中复合费米子边缘态的光谱学研究#
    Optical spectroscopy of composite fermion edge states in the fractional quantum Hall effect#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Maria Sebastian, Ashutosh Singh, Alexey Belyanin🔥 AI 99
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们表明，利用亚太赫兹光谱学可以选择性地探测和激发分数量子霍尔效应样品中的边缘态。借助复合费米子（CF）平均场框架，该框架将强关联的分数量子霍尔问题映射为有效的整数量子霍尔问题，我们计算了 Jain 填充分数序列的吸收光谱，其中同时包括体态和边缘态。复合费米子边缘态的吸收峰出现在毫米波至亚太赫兹范围内，例如在 GaAs 中、磁场为 B = 10 T 时位于 60 至 500 GHz；也就是说，相对于体复合费米子回旋频率，它们发生了蓝移，但仍显著低于相同磁场下整数量子霍尔回旋频率的尺度。吸收光谱中每一组得到分辨的峰的数目等于已填充 Lambda 能级的数目，从而为该分数提供指纹特征。边缘附近的反演对称性破缺会激活体内禁戒的光学跃迁，并在电偶极近似下实现二阶非线性过程。光谱的绝对频率尺度由复合费米子有效质量决定，而该质量完全由电子-电子相互作用产生，因此吸收光谱提供了对这一相互作用诱导质量的直接光学探测手段。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26521v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We show that edge states in fractional quantum Hall effect samples can be selectively probed and excited with sub-terahertz optical spectroscopy. Using the composite fermion (CF) mean-field framework, which maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall problem onto an effective integer quantum Hall problem, we calculate the absorbance spectrum for the Jain sequence of filling fractions including both bulk and edge states. The CF edge-state absorption peaks appear in the millimeter-wave to sub-terahertz range, e.g., 60-500 GHz at B = 10 T in GaAs, i.e. they are blueshifted with respect to the bulk CF cyclotron frequency but are well below the integer quantum Hall cyclotron frequency scale at the same magnetic fields. The number of resolved peaks in each series of the absorption spectrum counts the filled Lambda-levels and fingerprints the fraction. Inversion symmetry breaking near the edge activates optical transitions forbidden in the bulk and enables second-order nonlinear processes in electric-dipole approximation. The absolute frequency scale of the spectrum is set by the CF effective mass, which is generated entirely by electron-electron interactions, so the absorption spectrum provides a direct optical probe of this interaction-induced mass.

    合成磁场中耦合一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的涡旋晶格#
    Vortex lattices in coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates with a synthetic magnetic field#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Holly A. J. Middleton-Spencer, Rose Davies, David G. Reid, Anna Dalmasso, Theodore Kazanas, Hannah M. Price🔥 AI 97
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们研究了处于合成磁场中的耦合一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的平均场基态。由此得到的相互作用耦合线模型具有一个连续的空间方向和一个离散的空间方向，为在少腿梯子的物理与扩展涡旋晶格的物理之间进行受控插值提供了条件。对于两根导线，我们研究了类涡旋态、偏置密度态和类迈斯纳态，并考察系统有限的纵向尺寸如何改变它们之间的相变。随着导线数量增加，基态逐渐演化为扩展涡旋晶格。我们通过数值计算发现，离散方向上的周期性边界条件能够促进形成同号涡旋的交错阵列，其形态类似阿布里科索夫晶格；而在小尺寸有限系统中，开放边界会将涡旋限制在合成方向中心附近的若干行内。我们的结果探讨了有限尺寸效应和边界条件如何控制连续-离散量子流体中涡旋的产生及空间组织，这对于未来在隧穿耦合原子导线或合成维度中的实验具有参考意义。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26966v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We investigate the mean-field ground states of coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates subject to a synthetic magnetic field. The resulting interacting coupled-wire model has one continuous and one discrete spatial direction, providing a controlled way to interpolate between the physics of few-leg ladders and extended vortex lattices. For two wires, we study the vortex-like, biased-density, and Meissner-like states, exploring how the finite longitudinal size of the system modifies the transitions between them. Increasing the number of wires, the ground state evolves towards an extended vortex lattice. We find numerically that periodic boundary conditions in the discrete direction can favour staggered arrays of like-signed vortices resembling an Abrikosov lattice, while open boundaries in small finite-size systems confine the vortices into rows near the centre of the synthetic direction. Our results explore how finite size effects and boundary conditions govern the emergence and spatial organisation of vortices in continuous-discrete quantum fluids, with relevance to future experiments in tunnel-coupled atomic wires or with synthetic dimensions.

    超莫尔超晶格陈氏马赛克中的量子化输运#
    Quantized Transport through a Supermoiré Chern Mosaic#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Li-Qiao Xia, Aviram Uri, Zachary W. Gomez, Molly P. Andersen, Julian May-Mann, Kenji Watanabe, Takashi Taniguchi, Trithep Devakul, Yves H. Kwan, Pablo Jarillo-Herrero, Aaron Sharpe🔥 AI 97

    📄 摘要：魔角同向螺旋三层石墨烯——即三层石墨烯依次沿同一方向扭转约 1.8°——会弛豫为一个畴马赛克结构。在零磁场下，这些畴携带符号相反的谷分辨陈数，其边界上存在由无能隙导电模构成的网络。电荷通过该网络输运时，结果高度依赖于这些模如何连接和散射，因此很难实现良好量子化的输运。与这一预期相反，我们观测到一个从电荷中性点延伸出来的、由磁场诱导的陈能隙，其陈数为 C=-6；在该能隙中，霍尔电阻在 4.6 K 下量子化到预期值 -h/6e² 的 2% 以内。我们利用 Hofstadter 计算和轨道 Zeeman 计算解释了这一行为。计算表明，适度磁场会驱动谷选择性的拓扑转变。在转变之上，每个自旋-谷自由度中占据态的总陈数在相邻畴之间变得相同，因而畴壁模可能获得能隙。尽管中心价带的陈数在两类畴之间仍然不同，但所观测到的量子化输运证明体系存在全局能隙。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26252v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Magic-angle helical trilayer graphene---three graphene layers sequentially twisted in the same direction by sim1.8ⁱ̧rc---relaxes into a mosaic of domains that, at zero field, carry opposite valley-resolved Chern numbers, with boundaries hosting a network of gapless conducting modes. Charge transport through this network depends sensitively on how the modes connect and scatter, making well-quantized transport unlikely. Contrary to this expectation, we observe a field-induced Chern gap with Chern number C=-6 emanating from charge neutrality; in this gap, the Hall resistance is quantized to within 2% of the expected value, -h/6e², at 4.6 K. We explain this behavior using both Hofstadter and orbital Zeeman calculations, which show that a moderate magnetic field drives a valley-selective topological transition. Above the transition, the total Chern number of the occupied states in each spin-valley flavor becomes identical across neighboring domains, and the domain-wall modes can become gapped. Though the central valence-band Chern numbers still differ between the two domain types, the observed quantized transport attests to a global gap.

    通量工程量子线结中的拓扑保护完美交叉安德烈夫反射#
    Topologically protected perfect crossed Andreev reflection in flux-engineered quantum wire junctions#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Abhiram Soori🔥 AI 96
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：通过 Cooper 对分裂产生非局域纠缠电子对，对于固态量子信息处理至关重要。然而，由于电子隧穿（ET）和局域安德烈夫反射（AR）等竞争性输运过程的存在，分离出其背后的交叉安德烈夫反射（CAR）具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出一种可由磁通调控的四端正-超导结，实现确定性的、效率为 100% 的 CAR。在恰好为半个磁通量子（φ = π）时，精确的 Aharonov-Bohm 干涉和 Peierls 干涉分别从结构上禁止 ET 和 AR。通过调节中心结的跃迁，电子反射也可以被抑制为零。利用 Cauchy 辐角原理，我们证明这种抑制体现为量子化的拓扑绕数，从而保证 CAR 概率为 1 且受到拓扑保护。我们进一步表明，该区域以严格为正的交叉关联散粒噪声为特征，这为 Cooper 对分裂提供了明确无歧义的实验信号。此外，这种完美 CAR 在超导能隙内近似具有宽带特性，并且对结构无序具有显著鲁棒性，为确定性地产生非局域纠缠提供了一种高度耐受的架构。

    arXiv:2608.26702v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Generating non-locally entangled electron pairs via Cooper-pair splitting is vital for solid-state quantum information processing. However, isolating the underlying crossed Andreev reflection (CAR) is challenging due to competing transport processes like electron tunneling (ET) and local Andreev reflection (AR). Here, we propose a flux-tunable four-terminal normal metal-superconductor junction that achieves deterministic, 100% efficient CAR. We demonstrate that at exactly half a magnetic flux quantum (φ=π), exact destructive Aharonov-Bohm and Peierls interferences structurally forbid ET and AR respectively. By tuning the central junction hopping, electron reflection is also suppressed to zero. Using the Cauchy argument principle, we prove that this suppression manifests as a quantized topological winding number, guaranteeing a topologically protected unit CAR probability. We establish that this regime is characterized by a strictly positive cross-correlation shot noise, providing an unambiguous experimental signature of Cooper-pair splitting. Furthermore, this perfect CAR is nearly broadband within the superconducting gap and remarkably robust against structural disorder, offering a highly resilient architecture for deterministic nonlocal entanglement generation.

    集成硼掺杂金刚石微波天线和温度计的金刚石量子传感平台#
    Diamond quantum-sensing platform with integrated boron-doped diamond microwave antenna and thermometer#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Masahiro Ohkuma, Ryo Matsumoto, Shintaro Adachi, Shinobu Onoda, Kensei Terashima, Takao Watanabe, Yoshihiko Takano, Keigo Arai🔥 AI 94
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：低温条件下的宽场氮空位中心（NV）磁成像需要微波激发，并且需要可靠地了解传感区域附近的温度。在此，我们报告了一种集成式金刚石量子传感平台，该平台将 NV 中心阵列、硼掺杂金刚石（BDD）微波天线和温度计集成在同一块金刚石衬底上。BDD 天线为光学探测磁共振测量提供微波激发，而 BDD 温度计则监测 NV 传感区域附近的热环境。BDD 温度计探测到了激光诱导的局部升温，而安装在台架上的温度计未能清晰分辨这一升温。利用该平台，我们在记录温度的同时，对多个铜酸盐超导体的温度依赖迈斯纳响应进行了成像。这些结果表明，集成式 BDD—NV 平台为低温宽场磁成像提供了一种实用方案，能够同时实现集成式微波传输和局部测温。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27160v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Wide-field nitrogen-vacancy (NV) magnetic imaging at cryogenic temperatures requires microwave excitation and reliable knowledge of the temperature near the sensing region. Here, we report an integrated diamond quantum-sensing platform combining an ensemble of NV centers with a boron-doped diamond (BDD) microwave antenna and thermometer formed on the same diamond substrate. The BDD antenna provides microwave excitation for optically detected magnetic resonance measurements, and the BDD thermometer monitors the thermal environment near the NV sensing region. The BDD thermometer detected laser-induced local heating that was not clearly resolved by a stage-mounted thermometer. Using this platform, we imaged the temperature-dependent Meissner response of multiple cuprate superconductors while recording the temperature. These results demonstrate that the integrated BDD--NV platform provides a practical approach for cryogenic wide-field magnetic imaging with integrated microwave delivery and local thermometry.

    量子几何究竟有多量子？#
    How quantum is quantum geometry?#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ady Stern, Felix von Oppen🔥 AI 90
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：量子几何张量——即 Berry 曲率与量子度规——如今构成了大量可观测量的基础，从反常霍尔效应到平带的超流权重都包括在内。我们要问的是，这些可观测量中究竟哪些真正需要量子力学。为回答这一问题，我们研究了一个纯经典体系：一个携带经典磁矩 ℓ 的点粒子，该磁矩在依赖于动量的磁场 B(p) 中发生进动。在哈密顿经典力学框架内，ℓ 沿磁场方向的分量能够复现 Berry 曲率现象，而其进动的横向分量则能够复现量子度规现象。该粒子会获得位置展宽，其二阶矩就是度规；它还会获得轨道磁矩，更引人注目的是，会获得由位置依赖力产生的惯性质量，并由此在名义上无色散的体系中产生非零 Drude 权重。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26269v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The quantum geometric tensor - the Berry curvature together with the quantum metric - now underlies a long list of observables, from the anomalous Hall effect to the superfluid weight of a flat band. We ask which of these observables actually require quantum mechanics. To answer this question, we study a purely classical system: a point particle carrying a classical magnetic moment boldsymbolell that precesses in a momentum-dependent magnetic field B(p). Within Hamiltonian classical mechanics, the component of boldsymbolell along the field reproduces the Berry-curvature phenomena, while its precessing transverse component reproduces the quantum-metric phenomena. The particle acquires a position spread whose second moment is the metric, an orbital magnetic moment, and - most strikingly - an inertial mass generated by a position-dependent force, and with it a nonzero Drude weight in a system that is nominally dispersionless.

    💡 亮点：量子几何张量——即 Berry 曲率与量子度规——如今构成了大量可观测量的基础，从反常霍尔效应到平带的超流权重都包括在内。我们要问的是，这些可观测量中究竟哪些真正需要量子力学。为回答这一问题，我们研究了一个纯经典体系：一个携带经典磁矩 ℓ 的点粒子，该磁矩在依赖于动量的磁场 B(p) 中发生进动。在哈密顿经典力学框架内，ℓ 沿磁场方向的分量能够复现 Berry 曲率现象，而其进动的横向分量则能够复现量子度规现象。该粒子会获得位置展宽，其二阶矩就是度规；它还会获得轨道磁矩，更引…

    区分拓扑马约拉纳线与正常导线段的量子电容特征#
    Distinguishing Quantum Capacitance Signatures of a Topological Majorana Wire from a Normal Wire Segment#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Binayyak Bhusan Roy, Jay Deep Sau, Sumanta Tewari🔥 AI 82
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：马约拉纳零模（MZM）是预期存在于拓扑超导线两端、空间上彼此分离的近零能激发。量子点干涉仪可用于探测拓扑超导线的量子电容，而电容共振的位置和幅度能够提供有关马约拉纳零模的信息，其对磁通的依赖性则可探测与两个马约拉纳模的相干耦合。近期研究表明，无能隙，即 Δ=0 的导线段也能够通过阿哈罗诺夫—玻姆干涉产生磁通依赖的量子电容振荡，并且在适当调节下可以再现类似马约拉纳的响应。在此，我们表明这两种机制可以通过实验加以区分。在无能隙正常导线段中，两个依赖宇称的信号源自彼此分离的能量共振，这反映出体系通常不存在零能态。相比之下，在拓扑导线中，具有偶宇称和奇宇称的低能级在能量上近乎简并，因此两个宇称分支会保持在量子点势能对应的同一个较宽共振区域内，并且在独立改变量子点势能和导线化学势时仍然存在。我们的结果表明，实验观测到的量子电容响应，其判别依据并不是参数空间中某个经过优化的单一点处的特定磁通曲线，而是响应能否在包括量子点势能在内的多个独立可控参数的有限范围内持续存在。这种参数空间稳定性提供了一种直接方法，可排除无能隙正常导线段是所观测到的类马约拉纳响应来源的可能性。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27452v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZMs) are spatially separated, near-zero-energy excitations expected at the ends of a topological superconducting (TS) wire. A quantum-dot interferometer can be used to probe the quantum capacitance of the TS wire, and the location and magnitude of the capacitance resonances provide information about the MZMs, while their magnetic flux dependence probes coherent coupling to the two Majorana modes. It has been recently shown that, a gapless (i.e., Δ=0) wire segment can also exhibit flux-dependent quantum capacitance oscillations through Aharonov-Bohm interference and, with suitable tuning, can reproduce a Majorana-like response. Here we show that the two mechanisms can be distinguished experimentally. In the gapless normal wire segment, the two parity-dependent signals originate from separate energy resonances corresponding to the lack of generic zero-energy states. By contrast, in the topological wire, the pair of low energy levels with even and odd parity are nearly degenerate in energy, and therefore, the two parity branches remain within the same broader resonance region in the quantum-dot potential, and persist under independent variations of the dot potential and wire chemical potential. Our results show that the experimentally observed quantum capacitance response is distinguished not by a particular flux trace at one optimized point in parameter space, but by its persistence over a finite range of independently controlled parameters, including the quantum-dot potential. This parameter space stability provides a direct means of ruling out the gapless normal wire segment as the origin of the observed Majorana-like response.

    💡 亮点：马约拉纳零模（MZM）是预期存在于拓扑超导线两端、空间上彼此分离的近零能激发。量子点干涉仪可用于探测拓扑超导线的量子电容，而电容共振的位置和幅度能够提供有关马约拉纳零模的信息，其对磁通的依赖性则可探测与两个马约拉纳模的相干耦合。近期研究表明，无能隙，即 Δ=0 的导线段也能够通过阿哈罗诺夫—玻姆干涉产生磁通依赖的量子电容振荡，并且在适当调节下可以再现类似马约拉纳的响应。在此，我们表明这两种机制可以通过实验加以区分。在无能隙正常导线段中，两个依赖宇称的信号源自彼此分离的能量共振…

    平行磁场中拓扑 Dirac 半金属的非线性霍尔效应#
    Nonlinear Hall effect in topological Dirac semimetals in parallel magnetic field#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Maxim Dzero, Maxim Khodas, Alex Levchenko, Vladyslav Kozii
    相关度

    📄 摘要：我们计算了二维拓扑狄拉克半金属在外部面内磁场作用下的二次谐波响应。我们推导了 Wigner 分布函数所满足的量子动理学方程，并对其进行求解，以评估电流密度中二阶电场贡献。我们在广泛的模型参数范围内分析了贝里曲率偶极矩以及磁场诱导电流中的相关项。我们提出，可以通过测量拓扑绝缘体 SnTe 的表面态、WTe₂ 和 WSe₂ 单层，以及 Kondo 晶格材料 Ce₃Bi₄Pd₃ 在极低温下的反常霍尔电阻率随面内磁场的变化，对我们的理论进行实验检验。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2508.20159v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We compute the second-harmonic response of two-dimensional topological Dirac semimetals subjected to an external in-plane magnetic field. The quantum kinetic equation for the Wigner distribution function is derived and then solved to evaluate the second-order electric-field contributions to the current density. Both the Berry curvature dipole and the field-induced terms in the current are analyzed across a broad range of model parameters. We propose that our theory can be tested experimentally by measuring the dependence of the anomalous Hall resistivity on the in-plane magnetic field in the surface states of the topological insulator SnTe, in WTe₂ and WSe₂ monolayers, as well as in the Kondo lattice material Ce₃Bi₄Pd₃ at very low temperatures.

    💡 亮点：文章建立二维拓扑 Dirac 半金属面内磁场下的二阶输运理论，目标信号是二次谐波非线性霍尔电流。作者由 Wigner 分布函数推导并求解量子动力学方程，同时保留 Berry 曲率偶极与磁场诱导电流贡献。创新点是将两类机制放在统一参数扫描中比较，而非仅采用零场半经典偶极公式。摘要未给出具体材料、磁场强度、频率或非线性电导量值。

    二聚锯齿链中退禁闭自旋子的动力学特征#
    Dynamical signatures of deconfined spinons in dimerized sawtooth chains#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Nishan Ranabhat, Brandon B. Le, Seung-Hun Lee, Gia-Wei Chern🔥 AI 97
    相关度
    4

    📄 摘要：我们研究了锯齿链上海森堡模型在精确可解的价键固体（VBS）点及其附近的基态性质和动力学响应。我们采用具有U(1)对称性的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）和含时变分原理（TDVP）方法，计算二聚化相中的平衡诊断量以及零温动力学结构因子Szz(q,ω)。该相为一种价键有序态，其中每个三角形中由对称性等价的两条顶点-底边键发展出不相等的自旋关联；我们考察了接近连续型低相边界、精确VBS点以及接近一级型高边界时的情况。在每个区域中，动力学结构因子都是由其低能边缘上一条明亮谱带主导的宽连续谱，而单三重态激发能量ω≈JAB处的强度受到抑制。我们将该谱识别为退禁闭的双自旋子连续谱，其自旋子是两种简并单态覆盖之间的畴壁，即 kink 和 antikink。闭式自旋子色散关系确定了连续谱边界，并在全部三个区域中追踪跨越整个布里渊区的主导谱带；与此同时，在受限希尔伯特空间中进行的显式有限分离双kink计算再现了测得的强度分布。我们的结果为二聚化锯齿链中的分数量子化激发提供了微观图像，并与近期发现的Ti³⁺笼目氟化物相关；在这些材料中，强烈各向异性的交换相互作用能够产生锯齿链结构单元。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26347v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. We employ U(1)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) methods to compute equilibrium diagnostics and the zero-temperature dynamical structure factor (DSF) Szz(q,ømega) across the dimerized phase, a valence-bond-ordered state in which the two symmetry-equivalent apex--base bonds of each triangle develop unequal spin correlations, probing the approach to the continuous lower phase boundary, the exact VBS point, and the approach to the first-order upper boundary. In every regime, the DSF is a broad continuum dominated by a bright band at its lower edge, with the intensity at the one-triplon energy ømega simeq JAB suppressed. We identify the spectrum as a deconfined two-spinon continuum of kink and antikink domain walls between the two degenerate singlet coverings. Closed-form spinon dispersions fix the continuum edges and track the dominant band across the zone in all three regimes, while an explicit finite-separation two-kink calculation in a constrained Hilbert space reproduces the measured intensity distribution. Our results provide a microscopic picture of fractionalized excitations in the dimerized sawtooth chain and are relevant to the recently discovered Ti³⁺ kagome fluorides, where strongly anisotropic exchange interactions can generate sawtooth-chain building blocks.

    💡 亮点：作者用保持 U(1) 对称性的 DMRG 和含时变分原理计算二聚锯齿链 Heisenberg 模型的基态与零温 Szz(q,ω)。体系围绕精确可解价键固体点展开，并考察二聚相中对称键不等价导致的价键序。动力学结构因子被用来识别退禁闭自旋子的谱学特征，但摘要未给出峰位置、速度或临界参数。该文与团队自旋哈密顿量、蒙特卡洛和量子磁性研究有模型层面的交叉。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：团队已经使用有效自旋哈密顿量、蒙特卡洛和自旋动力学研究磁性、非共线磁序及自旋-晶格耦合，因此该文可作为低维量子磁性模型的数值参照。真实交叉限于 Heisenberg 相互作用、量子自旋激发和动力学结构因子；锯齿链并非团队背景列出的具体材料。摘要没有第一性原理参数、二维范德华磁体、Kitaev 相互作用或 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 项，不能把结果直接外推到真实磁性材料。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”补充训练/验证集，再比较《二聚锯齿链中退禁闭自旋子的动力学特征》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。
